  • 本期猴痘病毒(Mpox病毒)研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • 该研究报告称,猴痘 暴发 的管理存在伦理挑战,特别是在数据共享和患者隐私的背景下,但没有提供具体的数值结果。 [10.1093/ced/llag458]。
  • 与身体其他部位的皮肤病变相比,生殖器病变的愈合速度明显较慢,平均愈合时间为 21 ± 3.2 天,而周围病变的平均愈合时间为 13 ± 2.8 天 (p < 0.05)。此外,两名患者 (25%) 出现严重营养不良 (BMI < 16 kg/m2),这与明显更长的愈合时间相关,平均为 24 ± 2 天,而营养状况正常的患者为 19 ± 3 天 (p = 0.04)。
  • 总体而言,42.9%的家庭对人畜共患疾病的了解令人满意,63.6%的家庭持积极态度,11.7%的家庭表现出足够的预防措施。78.2% 的受访者将 猴痘 视为 人兽共患病,埃博拉为 66.6%,狂犬病为 39.1%。畜牧业家庭的知识得分高于非畜牧业家庭(p = 0.002)。在总体 KAP 的多变量分析中,大学教育(OR = 12.06;95% CI:1.54-94.18;p = 0.018)和饮用水获取情况(OR = 6.74;95% CI:3.91-11.60;p < 0.001)与令人满意的总体 KAP 呈正相关。
  • 更高的收入与更好的 猴痘 知识相关。 [10.1371/journal.pgph.0007383]。
  • This reporting window highlights the following research developments.
  • Tecovirimat did not significantly shorten the time to clinical resolution of skin lesions compared with placebo (HR 1.10, 95% CI 0.52-2.31, GRADE: moderate) or increase lesion resolution by day 28-29 (RR 1.00, 95% CI 0.92-1.08, GRADE: high).
  • The study reports that ethical challenges arise in the management of mpox outbreaks, particularly in the context of data sharing and patient privacy, with no specific numerical results provided. [10.1093/ced/llag458].
  • Genital lesions exhibited significantly slower healing compared to cutaneous lesions located on other body areas, with a mean healing time of 21 ± 3.2 days versus 13 ± 2.8 days for peripheral lesions (p < 0.05).
  • Overall, 42.9% of households demonstrated satisfactory knowledge of zoonotic diseases, 63.6% had favorable attitudes, and 11.7% demonstrated adequate preventive practices.

📰 本期新闻动态 / News Brief

  • 本期猴痘病毒(Mpox病毒)新闻集中呈现以下动态。
  • 一名男子被确认感染猴痘病毒,并被送往莫斯科州多莫杰多沃医院住院治疗,标志着俄罗斯在该月内第三例莫斯科地区确诊病例,以及自2022年暴发疫情以来的第八例确诊病例。该事件由多莫杰多沃医院的官方通讯渠道报告。
  • 在斯坦尼斯拉乌斯县报告了4例近期Clade I 猴痘病例,引发了对一种历史上与国际旅行相关病毒可能在社区中传播的担忧。加州公共卫生部已确认其中2例为Clade I,其余2例仍在进一步检测中。 [news-e23c2e177c48f02e]。
  • 斯坦尼斯劳斯县报告了四例猴痘病例,其中两例被确认为I系(N27,N28,N29)。 [news-b6fc716ff831601c]。
  • 其中2例被确认为I系,另外2例仍在检测中。这些病例由斯坦尼斯拉乌斯县公共卫生部门和加利福尼亚州公共卫生部报告。 [news-5de1c5765bdfdfec]。
  • This reporting window highlights the following Mpox virus news developments.
  • A man was confirmed positive for mpox and hospitalized at Domodedovo Hospital in Moscow Oblast, marking Russia's third confirmed Moscow-region case in a single month and its eighth since the 2022 outbreak.
  • Four recent cases of Clade I mpox were reported in Stanislaus County, raising concerns about possible community transmission of a strain historically linked to international travel. [news-e23c2e177c48f02e].
  • Four mpox cases were reported in Stanislaus County, with two confirmed as clade I (N27, N28, N29). [news-b6fc716ff831601c].
  • Two of the cases are confirmed as Clade I, while the other two are still being tested. The cases are reported by the Stanislaus County Public Health and the California Department of Public Health. [news-5de1c5765bdfdfec].

公共卫生、患者保密和 猴痘:道德困境

Public health, patient confidentiality and mpox: an ethical dilemma
作者： Harini Elankhumaren, Frances Emmanuel, Emmanuel Odega
摘要中文翻译哈里尼·埃兰库马伦、弗朗西斯·伊曼纽尔、伊曼纽尔·奥德加;公共卫生、患者保密和 猴痘:道德困境,临床和实验 Derma
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了 猴痘 疾病背景下公共卫生需求和患者保密之间的伦理困境,强调需要采取平衡的方法来管理传染病 暴发。
设计与对象
该研究是一项定性分析,重点关注 猴痘 疾病管理中的伦理考虑,在公共卫生和临床实践领域进行,没有具体提及样本量或人口统计数据。
核心方法
这些方法涉及对与 猴痘 相关的道德框架和案例研究的审查,重点是平衡公共卫生要求与患者个人保密权。
主要结果
该研究报告称,猴痘 暴发 的管理存在伦理挑战,特别是在数据共享和患者隐私的背景下,但没有提供具体的数值结果。
解释与创新
作者认为这些挑战对于制定传染病管理的道德准则具有重要意义,为公共卫生与个人权利之间的紧张关系提供了新的视角。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以为制定管理 猴痘 暴发 的道德指南提供信息,确保尊重患者机密,同时支持有效的公共卫生干预措施。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其定性性质和缺乏定量数据而受到限制,导致根据所提供的证据进行适度的置信度评估。

Public health, patient confidentiality and mpox: an ethical dilemma

Authors: Harini Elankhumaren, Frances Emmanuel, Emmanuel Odega
Original abstract (English)
Harini Elankhumaren, Frances Emmanuel, Emmanuel Odega; Public health, patient confidentiality and mpox: an ethical dilemma, Clinical and Experimental Derma
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the ethical dilemma between public health needs and patient confidentiality in the context of mpox disease, highlighting the need for a balanced approach to managing infectious disease outbreaks.
Design and population
The study is a qualitative analysis focusing on ethical considerations in mpox disease management, conducted in the field of public health and clinical practice, with no specific mention of sample size or population demographics.
Core methods
The methods involve a review of ethical frameworks and case studies related to mpox, with an emphasis on balancing public health requirements against individual patient confidentiality rights.
Main results
The study reports that ethical challenges arise in the management of mpox outbreaks, particularly in the context of data sharing and patient privacy, with no specific numerical results provided.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret these challenges as significant for shaping ethical guidelines in infectious disease management, offering a novel perspective on the tension between public health and individual rights.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of ethical guidelines for managing mpox outbreaks, ensuring that patient confidentiality is respected while supporting effective public health interventions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its qualitative nature and lack of quantitative data, resulting in a moderate confidence assessment based on the provided evidence.

2022-2025 年巴西 猴痘 的流行病学轨迹:地理整合和流行病负担的证据。

Epidemiological trajectory of Mpox in Brazil, 2022-2025: evidence of geographic consolidation and syndemic burden.
作者： Gabriel do Nascimento Soares, Camila Innocente-Alves, Ana Luiza Marques, Tatiane da Silva Dal Pizzol, Fabrício S Campos, Lucélia Santi, Walter Orlando Beys-da-Silva
摘要中文翻译在巴西这个生态和社会连通性较高的国家,猴痘 已从 暴发 过渡到持续的 传播。我们利用巴西卫生部报告的 14 475 例实验室确诊病例,分析了 2022 年至 2025 年 猴痘 的流行病学轨迹。我们的研究结果表明 发病率 的地理整合,其中在东南部和中西部地区观察到最高的 发病率。该流行病对男性(93.0%)尤其是年轻人的影响尤为严重,传播 主要与男男性行为网络(69.1%)有关。确定了合并感染的巨大负担:47.1% 的报告病例发生在艾滋病毒感染者中,30.8% 患有梅毒。临床上,大多数患者表现为皮肤表现(91.3%)。这些发现强调了影响巴西 猴痘 传播 的流行病学和社会决定因素的融合。加强综合 监测 系统并确保公平、无耻辱地获得预防和护理对于减少 传播 和减轻更广泛传播的风险至关重要。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在了解巴西 猴痘 的流行病学轨迹,强调从 暴发 到持续 传播 的转变,以及解决由于高度生态和社会连通性而日益增长的公共卫生问题的必要性。
设计与对象
该研究利用巴西卫生部报告的 14 475 例实验室确诊病例,分析了 2022 年至 2025 年 猴痘 的流行病学轨迹,重点关注受影响个体的地理模式和人口特征。
核心方法
研究人员使用巴西卫生部的实验室确诊病例数据来检查 猴痘 病例的地理和人口分布,包括 发病率 比率和 传播 网络。
主要结果
研究结果表明 猴痘 发病率 的地理整合,其中在东南部和中西部地区观察到最高的 发病率。此外,93.0% 的病例为男性,69.1% 与男男性行为网络有关。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为流行病学和社会决定因素在巴西 猴痘 传播 中趋同的证据,强调了有针对性的公共卫生干预措施的必要性。
科研与公卫意义
结果强调了加强综合 监测 系统并确保公平获得预防和护理的重要性,以减少 传播 并减轻更广泛的传播风险。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于其依赖于实验室确诊病例,可能无法捕获 猴痘 的所有实例,并且可能存在地理和人口统计学偏差,导致研究结果的可信度中等。

Epidemiological trajectory of Mpox in Brazil, 2022-2025: evidence of geographic consolidation and syndemic burden.

Authors: Gabriel do Nascimento Soares, Camila Innocente-Alves, Ana Luiza Marques, Tatiane da Silva Dal Pizzol, Fabrício S Campos, Lucélia Santi, Walter Orlando Beys-da-Silva
Original abstract (English)
Mpox has transitioned from outbreak to sustained transmission in Brazil, a country with high ecological and social connectivity. We analysed the epidemiological trajectory of mpox during 2022-2025 using 14 475 laboratory-confirmed cases reported by the Brazilian Ministry of Health. Our findings indicate geographical consolidation of transmission, with the highest incidence observed in the southeast and midwest regions. The epidemic disproportionately affects men (93.0%), particularly young adults, with transmission primarily associated with networks of men who have sex with men (69.1%). A substantial burden of co-infections was identified: 47.1% of reported cases occurred in persons living with HIV and 30.8% had syphilis. Clinically, most patients presented with cutaneous manifestations (91.3%). These findings highlight the convergence of epidemiological and social determinants shaping mpox transmission in Brazil. Strengthening integrated surveillance systems and ensuring equitable, stigma-free access to prevention and care are critical to reducing transmission and mitigating the risk of broader dissemination.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand the epidemiological trajectory of mpox in Brazil, highlighting the transition from outbreak to sustained transmission and the need to address the growing public health concern due to high ecological and social connectivity.
Design and population
The study analysed the epidemiological trajectory of mpox during 2022-2025 using 14 475 laboratory-confirmed cases reported by the Brazilian Ministry of Health, focusing on geographic patterns and demographic characteristics of affected individuals.
Core methods
The researchers used laboratory-confirmed case data from the Brazilian Ministry of Health to examine the geographic and demographic distribution of mpox cases, including incidence rates and transmission networks.
Main results
The findings indicate geographical consolidation of mpox transmission, with the highest incidence observed in the southeast and midwest regions. Additionally, 93.0% of cases were in men, and 69.1% were linked to networks of men who have sex with men.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as evidence of the convergence of epidemiological and social determinants in shaping mpox transmission in Brazil, highlighting the need for targeted public health interventions.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results underscore the importance of strengthening integrated surveillance systems and ensuring equitable access to prevention and care to reduce transmission and mitigate broader dissemination risks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on laboratory-confirmed cases, which may not capture all instances of mpox, and the potential for geographic and demographic biases, resulting in moderate confidence in the findings.

猴痘病毒转录组结构的异构体解析时间重塑

Isoform-Resolved Temporal Remodeling of Transcriptome Architecture in Monkeypox Virus
作者： Gergely Ármin Nagy, Balázs Kakuk, Zsolt Csabai, Ákos Dörmő, István Prazsák, Daniel Růžek, Jiri Holoubek, Gábor Kemenesi, Dóra Tombácz, Zsolt Boldogkői
摘要中文翻译摘要:我们重新分析了先前生成的来自六个感染时间点的复制的 Oxford Nanopore 直接 cDNA 测序数据,以及基因表达的上限分析和天然直接 RNA 测序,以重建全长猴痘病毒转录本并表​​征其时间调控。使用独立的 5' 和 3' 端证据过滤候选转录模型的广泛目录,包括候选 5' Poly(A) 相关结构,以定义保守的分析集。转录本解析的概况揭示了一个超出基因水平丰度的结构化时间程序。宿主-病毒相互作用、转录和核苷酸代谢基因在早期发挥了重要作用,而病毒粒子结构和形态发生基因在后期占主导地位。转录起始和终止位点丰度谱随着时间的推移发生了很大的变化,许多基因在替代的全长转录物结构中重新分布。转录本的差异使用在 宿主 病毒相互作用基因中尤为突出,但在病毒体结构和形态发生基因中却有所减少。候选 5'poly(A) 相关转录本的相对贡献也有所增加,并显示出位点特异性模式。在我们之前的终点图的基础上,复制的直接 cDNA 时间过程能够对基因丰度、转录本末端动态和亚型组成进行时间量化。总之,这些结果表明猴痘病毒基因调控涉及基因输出、转录边界和全长转录组结构的协调重塑。重要性 猴痘病毒是一种新兴的人类病原体,具有持续人传人 传播 的能力,因此详细了解其分子生物学变得越来越重要。病毒复制取决于协调的基因表达,定义病毒基因转录的时间和方式对于理解感染至关重要。完整的转录本结构提供了基因水平测量无法捕获的信息,因为替代的 RNA 形式在调控区域、编码潜力和翻译能力方面可能有所不同。因此,解决这些转录本的组织和时间使用有助于揭示病毒如何控制蛋白质产生以及与 宿主 细胞相互作用。猴痘病毒基因调控的转录水平视图为痘病毒生物学的机制研究提供了基础,并可能有助于识别与 抗病毒药物 干预相关的调控过程或病毒功能。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在了解猴痘病毒的分子生物学,特别是其基因调控机制,因为它是一种新兴的人类病原体,具有持续人际传播 传播 的潜力。这项研究的重要性在于需要定义病毒基因转录的时间和方式,以更好地了解感染过程。
设计与对象
该研究重新分析了先前生成的来自六个感染时间点的复制的 Oxford Nanopore 直接 cDNA 测序数据,以及基因表达的上限分析和天然直接 RNA 测序,以重建全长猴痘病毒转录本并表​​征其时间调控。
核心方法
使用独立的 5' 和 3' 端证据过滤候选转录模型的广泛目录,包括候选 5' Poly(A) 相关结构,以定义保守的分析集。复制的直接 cDNA 时间过程能够对基因丰度、转录本末端动态和亚型组成进行时间量化。
主要结果
转录本解析的概况揭示了一个超出基因水平丰度的结构化时间程序。候选 5'poly(A) 相关转录本的相对贡献也有所增加,并显示出位点特异性模式。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,猴痘病毒基因调控涉及基因输出、转录边界和全长转录组结构的协调重塑,为病毒转录动力学提供了新的视角。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果为痘病毒生物学机制研究奠定了基础,并可能有助于识别与 抗病毒药物 干预相关的调控过程或病毒功能,特别是在 猴痘 疾病的背景下。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于对先前生成的数据的重新分析,这可能会限制研究结果的新颖性。然而,多种测序方法的使用和转录模型的独立过滤为结果提供了中等程度的可信度。

Isoform-Resolved Temporal Remodeling of Transcriptome Architecture in Monkeypox Virus

Authors: Gergely Ármin Nagy, Balázs Kakuk, Zsolt Csabai, Ákos Dörmő, István Prazsák, Daniel Růžek, Jiri Holoubek, Gábor Kemenesi, Dóra Tombácz, Zsolt Boldogkői
Original abstract (English)
Abstract We reanalyzed previously generated replicated Oxford Nanopore direct cDNA sequencing data from six infection time points, together with cap analysis of gene expression and native direct RNA sequencing, to reconstruct full-length monkeypox virus transcripts and characterize their temporal regulation. A broad catalogue of candidate transcript models, including candidate 5′ poly(A)-associated structures, was filtered with independent 5′- and 3′-end evidence to define conservative analysis sets. Transcript-resolved profiles revealed a structured temporal program extending beyond gene-level abundance. Host-virus interaction, transcription, and nucleotide-metabolism genes contributed strongly early, whereas virion structure and morphogenesis genes dominated later. Transcription start and end site abundance profiles changed extensively over time, and many genes redistributed among alternative full-length transcript structures. Differential transcript usage was particularly prominent among host-virus interaction genes but depleted among virion structure and morphogenesis genes. Candidate 5′ poly(A)-associated transcripts also increased in relative contribution and showed locus-specific patterns. Building on our previous endpoint map, the replicated direct cDNA time course enabled temporal quantification of gene abundance, transcript-end dynamics, and isoform composition. Together, these results show that Monkeypox virus gene regulation involves coordinated remodeling of gene output, transcript boundaries, and full-length transcriptome architecture. Importance Monkeypox virus is an emerging human pathogen with the capacity for sustained human-to-human transmission, making a detailed understanding of its molecular biology increasingly important. Viral replication depends on coordinated gene expression, and defining when and how viral genes are transcribed is essential for understanding infection. Complete transcript structures provide information that gene-level measurements cannot capture because alternative RNA forms can differ in regulatory regions, coding potential, and capacity for translation. Resolving the organization and temporal use of these transcripts therefore helps reveal how the virus controls protein production and interacts with the host cell. A transcript-level view of monkeypox virus gene regulation provides a foundation for mechanistic studies of poxvirus biology and may help identify regulatory processes or viral functions relevant to antiviral intervention.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand the molecular biology of monkeypox virus, particularly its gene regulation mechanisms, as it is an emerging human pathogen with the potential for sustained human-to-human transmission. The importance of this research lies in the need to define when and how viral genes are transcribed to better understand infection processes.
Design and population
The study reanalyzed previously generated replicated Oxford Nanopore direct cDNA sequencing data from six infection time points, along with cap analysis of gene expression and native direct RNA sequencing, to reconstruct full-length monkeypox virus transcripts and characterize their temporal regulation.
Core methods
A broad catalogue of candidate transcript models, including candidate 5′ poly(A)-associated structures, was filtered with independent 5′- and 3′-end evidence to define conservative analysis sets. The replicated direct cDNA time course enabled temporal quantification of gene abundance, transcript-end dynamics, and isoform composition.
Main results
Transcript-resolved profiles revealed a structured temporal program extending beyond gene-level abundance. Candidate 5′ poly(A)-associated transcripts also increased in relative contribution and showed locus-specific patterns.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate that Monkeypox virus gene regulation involves coordinated remodeling of gene output, transcript boundaries, and full-length transcriptome architecture, providing a new perspective on viral transcriptional dynamics.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results offer a foundation for mechanistic studies of poxvirus biology and may help identify regulatory processes or viral functions relevant to antiviral intervention, particularly in the context of mpox disease.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on reanalysis of previously generated data, which may limit the novelty of the findings. However, the use of multiple sequencing methods and independent filtering of transcript models provides a moderate level of confidence in the results.

猴痘 传播 热点的地理空间分析和预测建模:以尼日利亚奥贡州为例

Geospatial analysis and predictive modelling of mpox transmission hotspots: a case study of Ogun State, Nigeria
作者： Adesina Simon Sodiya, Samuel Onuche-Ojo Egwu, Dada Olaniyi Aborisade, Saheed Akinbowale, Mary Idowu Olasoju, Dada O. Aborisade
摘要中文翻译猴痘 是尼日利亚反复出现的公共卫生威胁,并于 2024 年 8 月被重新宣布为国际关注的突发公共卫生事件。尼日利亚的综合疾病 猴痘 和响应系统在地方政府地区 (LGA) 层面运行,但发布的 猴痘 风险模型仅在州层面解决风险,迫使异构地方政府地区进行统一部署。本研究开发并评估了奥贡州 20 个 LGA 的 LGA 分辨率地理空间风险层,并将其作为概念验证集成到实时 监测 平台中。40 例实验室确诊病例(NCDC,2022 年)和 151 例疑似病例(奥贡州卫生部,2022 年至 2024 年)的州级记录,总共 191 例,在空间上分解为 189 个伪存在点,并使用最大熵、随机森林和极端梯度提升的集合建模,超过 100 个引导重复。在随机保留下(AUC 0.77;博伊斯指数 0.99),歧视性良好,但在空间阻塞交叉验证(AUC 0.57)下,歧视性下降,表明该层适合于层之间的粗略分类,而不是层内排名。沿阿贝奥库塔南和伊杰布-雷莫轴线的中央走廊,有 7 个地方政府行政区被列为高风险,6 个为中风险,7 个为低风险。分层层导出为具有特定于层的操作包的 GeoJSON,并集成到 IDS-Master 平台中,作为从空间 流行病学 到常规公共卫生 流行病学 的概念验证途径。由于 189 个点是从总计数中分解出来的,而不是单独进行地理定位的,因此这些层应被解读为分诊的空间先验,而不是已确认的病例位置。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
猴痘 是尼日利亚反复出现的公共卫生威胁,并于 2024 年 8 月被重新宣布为国际关注的突发公共卫生事件。尼日利亚的综合疾病 猴痘 和响应系统在地方政府地区 (LGA) 层面运行,但发布的 猴痘 风险模型仅在州层面解决风险,迫使异构地方政府地区进行统一部署。
设计与对象
本研究开发并评估了奥贡州 20 个 LGA 的 LGA 分辨率地理空间风险层,并将其作为概念验证集成到实时 监测 平台中。40 例实验室确诊病例(NCDC,2022 年)和 151 例疑似病例(奥贡州卫生部,2022 年至 2024 年)的州级记录,总共 191 例,在空间上分解为 189 个伪存在点,并使用最大熵、随机森林和极端梯度提升的集合建模,超过 100 个引导重复。
核心方法
由于 189 个点是从总计数中分解出来的,而不是单独进行地理定位的,因此这些层应被解读为分诊的空间先验,而不是已确认的病例位置。
主要结果
在随机保留下(AUC 0.77;博伊斯指数 0.99),区分度良好,但在空间阻塞交叉验证(AUC 0.57)下,辨别力下降,表明该层适合于层之间的粗略分类,而不是层内排名。沿阿贝奥库塔南和伊杰布-雷莫轴线的中央走廊,有 7 个地方政府行政区被列为高风险,6 个为中风险,7 个为低风险。
解释与创新
分层层导出为具有特定于层的操作包的 GeoJSON,并集成到 IDS-Master 平台中,作为从空间 流行病学 到常规公共卫生 流行病学 的概念验证途径。该研究提供了一种在 LGA 层面完善风险评估的方法,可以改善有针对性的公共卫生干预措施。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以为地方提供更精确的监测和应对策略,增强有效分配资源的能力。这种方法可以作为具有类似流行病学挑战的其他地区的模型。
局限与证据强度
研究结果表明,该模型在粗略分类方面表现良好,但在层内排名方面可能不可靠,这表明需要进一步细化和验证。证据是温和的,因为它依赖于空间分类数据而不是个案地理位置。

Geospatial analysis and predictive modelling of mpox transmission hotspots: a case study of Ogun State, Nigeria

Authors: Adesina Simon Sodiya, Samuel Onuche-Ojo Egwu, Dada Olaniyi Aborisade, Saheed Akinbowale, Mary Idowu Olasoju, Dada O. Aborisade
Original abstract (English)
Mpox is a recurring public health threat in Nigeria and was re-declared a Public Health Emergency of International Concern in August 2024. Nigeria's Integrated Disease Surveillance and Response system operates at the Local Government Area (LGA) level, yet published mpox risk models resolve risk only at state level, forcing uniform deployment across heterogeneous LGAs. This study developed and evaluated an LGA-resolution geospatial risk layer for the 20 LGAs of Ogun State and integrated it, as a proof of concept, into a live surveillance platform. State-level records of 40 laboratory-confirmed cases (NCDC, 2022) and 151 suspected cases (Ogun State Ministry of Health, 2022–2024), 191 in total, were spatially disaggregated into 189 pseudo-presence points and modelled with an ensemble of maximum entropy, random forest, and extreme gradient boosting over 100 bootstrap replicates. Discrimination was good under random hold-out (AUC 0.77; Boyce index 0.99) but fell under spatially blocked cross-validation (AUC 0.57), indicating that the layer is suited to coarse triage between tiers rather than within-tier ranking. Seven LGAs were classified high risk along a central corridor anchored on Abeokuta South and the Ijebu–Remo axis, six medium, and seven low. The tiered layer was exported as GeoJSON with tier-specific action packages and integrated into the IDS-Master platform as a proof-of-concept pathway from spatial epidemiology to routine public health surveillance. Because the 189 points were disaggregated from aggregate counts rather than individually geolocated, the tiers should be read as a spatial prior for triage rather than as confirmed case locations.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Mpox is a recurring public health threat in Nigeria and was re-declared a Public Health Emergency of International Concern in August 2024. Nigeria's Integrated Disease Surveillance and Response system operates at the Local Government Area (LGA) level, yet published mpox risk models resolve risk only at state level, forcing uniform deployment across heterogeneous LGAs.
Design and population
This study developed and evaluated an LGA-resolution geospatial risk layer for the 20 LGAs of Ogun State and integrated it, as a proof of concept, into a live surveillance platform. State-level records of 40 laboratory-confirmed cases (NCDC, 2022) and 151 suspected cases (Ogun State Ministry of Health, 2022–2024), 191 in total, were spatially disaggregated into 189 pseudo-presence points and modelled with an ensemble of maximum entropy, random forest, and extreme gradient boosting over 100 bootstrap replicates.
Core methods
Because the 189 points were disaggregated from aggregate counts rather than individually geolocated, the tiers should be read as a spatial prior for triage rather than as confirmed case locations.
Main results
Discrimination was good under random hold-out (AUC 0.77; Boyce index 0.99) but fell under spatially blocked cross-validation (AUC 0.57), indicating that the layer is suited to coarse triage between tiers rather than within-tier ranking. Seven LGAs were classified high risk along a central corridor anchored on Abeokuta South and the Ijebu–Remo axis, six medium, and seven low.
Interpretation and novelty
The tiered layer was exported as GeoJSON with tier-specific action packages and integrated into the IDS-Master platform as a proof-of-concept pathway from spatial epidemiology to routine public health surveillance. The study provides a method for refining risk assessment at the LGA level, which could improve targeted public health interventions.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform more precise surveillance and response strategies at the local level, enhancing the ability to allocate resources effectively. This approach may serve as a model for other regions with similar epidemiological challenges.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's results indicate that the model performs well for coarse triage but may not be reliable for within-tier ranking, suggesting that further refinement and validation are needed. The evidence is moderate as it relies on spatially disaggregated data rather than individual case geolocation.

2025 年刚果民主共和国布卡武家庭对重点人畜共患疾病(猴痘、埃博拉和狂犬病)的知识、态度和做法以及相关因素

Knowledge, attitudes, and practices regarding priority zoonotic diseases (Mpox, Ebola, and Rabies) and associated factors among households in Bukavu, Democratic Republic of the Congo, 2025
作者： Arnold Kwibuka Murangaza, Chiribagula Zalinga Christian, Christian Mugisho Byamungu, Hugues Cakirwa Mandevu, Gentil Mwema Kisangani, Rosine Bigirinama Nshobole, Christian Zalinga Chiribagula, Christian Byamungu Mugisho, Rosine Nshobole Bigirinama, Murangaza, A. K.
摘要中文翻译摘要背景:人畜共患疾病是刚果民主共和国 (DRC) 的主要公共卫生问题,狂犬病、埃博拉病毒和 猴痘 等疾病对该国构成持续的健康风险。家庭知识、态度和实践(KAP)在预防人畜共患疾病方面发挥着重要作用 传播。本研究评估了刚果民主共和国南基伍省布卡武的家庭对重点人畜共患疾病的知识、态度和做法,并确定了与总体 KAP 满意度相关的因素。方法:2025 年 9 月 1 日至 15 日在布卡武的巴吉拉和卡杜图卫生区进行了一项横断面研究。采用两阶段整群抽样方法,共抽取了 634 户家庭。使用 KoboCollect 管理的结构化调查问卷收集数据,并使用 Stata 版本 17 进行分析。调查问卷评估了有关埃博拉病毒、猴痘 和狂犬病的知识、态度和做法。知识使用 17 个项目进行测量,态度使用五点李克特量表项目进行测量,实践使用五个行为项目进行测量。至少 70% 的分数被列为满意。使用描述性统计、组间比较以及双变量和多变量逻辑回归分析。在适当的时候使用非参数检验。相关性以比值比 (OR) 和 95% 置信区间 (CI) 报告,p < 0.05 被认为具有统计显着性。结果:总体而言,42.9%的家庭对人畜共患疾病的了解令人满意,63.6%的家庭持良好态度,11.7%的家庭表现出足够的预防措施。78.2% 的受访者将 猴痘 视为 人兽共患病,埃博拉为 66.6%,狂犬病为 39.1%。畜牧业家庭的知识得分高于非畜牧业家庭(p = 0.002)。在总体 KAP 的多变量分析中,大学教育(OR = 12.06;95% CI:1.54-94.18;p = 0.018)和饮用水获取情况(OR = 6.74;95% CI:3.91-11.60;p < 0.001)与令人满意的总体 KAP 呈正相关。结论:布卡武家庭对人畜共患疾病预防普遍持积极态度,但令人满意的知识普及程度较低,适当的预防措施也不常见。干预措施应通过有针对性的风险沟通和健康教育,以及改善饮用水和卫生设施的获取,帮助将认识和积极态度转化为实际的预防行为。涉及人类、动物和环境健康利益相关者的协调一致的“同一个健康”方法可以加强家庭层面对重点人畜共患疾病的预防。研究结果可以帮助为当地制定预防策略提供信息,并确定未来基于社区的研究的优先事项。关键词:人畜共患病;知识、态度和实践;一个健康;布卡武;刚果民主共和国
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估刚果民主共和国布卡武家庭对重点人畜共患疾病(包括 猴痘、埃博拉病毒和狂犬病)的知识、态度和做法 (KAP),以确定与总体 KAP 令人满意相关的因素。人畜共患疾病是刚果民主共和国的一个主要公共卫生问题,狂犬病、埃博拉病毒和 猴痘 等疾病持续构成健康风险。
设计与对象
2025 年 9 月 1 日至 15 日在刚果民主共和国南基伍省布卡武的巴吉拉和卡杜图卫生区进行了一项横断面研究。采用两阶段整群抽样方法,共抽取了 634 户家庭。
核心方法
使用 KoboCollect 管理的结构化调查问卷收集数据,并使用 Stata 版本 17 进行分析。调查问卷评估了有关埃博拉病毒、猴痘 和狂犬病的知识、态度和做法。知识使用 17 个项目进行测量,态度使用五点李克特量表项目进行测量,实践使用五个行为项目进行测量。至少 70% 的分数被列为满意。使用描述性统计、组间比较以及双变量和多变量逻辑回归分析。在适当的时候使用非参数检验。
主要结果
总体而言,42.9%的家庭对人畜共患疾病的了解令人满意,63.6%的家庭持积极态度,11.7%的家庭表现出足够的预防措施。78.2% 的受访者将 猴痘 视为 人兽共患病,埃博拉为 66.6%,狂犬病为 39.1%。畜牧业家庭的知识得分高于非畜牧业家庭(p = 0.002)。在总体 KAP 的多变量分析中,大学教育(OR = 12.06;95% CI:1.54-94.18;p = 0.018)和饮用水获取情况(OR = 6.74;95% CI:3.91-11.60;p < 0.001)与令人满意的总体 KAP 呈正相关。
解释与创新
作者解释说,布卡武的家庭普遍对人畜共患疾病预防持积极态度,但令人满意的知识普及程度较低,适当的预防措施也不常见。该研究确定了与总体 KAP 满意度呈正相关的具体因素,例如大学教育和饮用水的获取。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以帮助为当地制定预防策略提供信息,并确定未来基于社区的研究的优先事项。干预措施应通过有针对性的风险沟通和健康教育,以及改善饮用水和卫生设施的获取,帮助将认识和积极态度转化为实际的预防行为。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到横断面设计的限制,无法确定因果关系,并且可能存在自我报告偏差。由于该研究的观察性质和缺乏纵向数据,证据强度中等。

Knowledge, attitudes, and practices regarding priority zoonotic diseases (Mpox, Ebola, and Rabies) and associated factors among households in Bukavu, Democratic Republic of the Congo, 2025

Authors: Arnold Kwibuka Murangaza, Chiribagula Zalinga Christian, Christian Mugisho Byamungu, Hugues Cakirwa Mandevu, Gentil Mwema Kisangani, Rosine Bigirinama Nshobole, Christian Zalinga Chiribagula, Christian Byamungu Mugisho, Rosine Nshobole Bigirinama, Murangaza, A. K.
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background: Zoonotic diseases are a major public health concern in the Democratic Republic of the Congo (DRC), where diseases such as rabies, Ebola, and Mpox pose ongoing health risks. Household knowledge, attitudes, and practices (KAP) play an important role in preventing zoonotic disease transmission. This study assessed household knowledge, attitudes, and practices regarding priority zoonotic diseases in Bukavu, South Kivu Province, DRC, and identified factors associated with satisfactory overall KAP. Methods: A cross-sectional study was conducted from September 1 to 15, 2025, in the Bagira and Kadutu health zones of Bukavu. A total of 634 households were selected using two-stage cluster sampling. Data were collected using a structured questionnaire administered through KoboCollect and analyzed using Stata version 17. The questionnaire assessed knowledge, attitudes, and practices concerning Ebola, Mpox, and rabies. Knowledge was measured using 17 items, attitudes using five-point Likert-scale items, and practices using five behavioral items. Scores of at least 70% were classified as satisfactory. Descriptive statistics, group comparisons, and bivariate and multivariable logistic regression analyses were used. Nonparametric tests were used when appropriate. Associations were reported as odds ratios (ORs) with 95% confidence intervals (CIs), with p < 0.05 considered statistically significant. Results: Overall, 42.9% of households demonstrated satisfactory knowledge of zoonotic diseases, 63.6% had favorable attitudes, and 11.7% demonstrated adequate preventive practices. Mpox was recognized as a zoonosis by 78.2% of respondents, Ebola by 66.6%, and rabies by 39.1%. Knowledge scores were higher among livestock-farming than non-livestock-farming households (p = 0.002). In the multivariable analysis of overall KAP, university-level education (OR = 12.06; 95% CI: 1.54-94.18; p = 0.018) and access to drinking water (OR = 6.74; 95% CI: 3.91-11.60; p < 0.001) were positively associated with satisfactory overall KAP. Conclusions: Households in Bukavu reported generally favorable attitudes toward zoonotic disease prevention, but satisfactory knowledge was less widespread and adequate preventive practices were uncommon. Interventions should help translate awareness and favorable attitudes into practical preventive behaviors through targeted risk communication and health education, alongside improvements in access to drinking water and sanitation. A coordinated One Health approach involving human, animal, and environmental health stakeholders may strengthen household-level prevention of priority zoonotic diseases. The findings can help inform locally tailored prevention strategies and identify priorities for future community-based research. Keywords: Zoonoses; Knowledge, Attitudes, and Practices; One Health; Bukavu; Democratic Republic of the Congo
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to assess household knowledge, attitudes, and practices (KAP) regarding priority zoonotic diseases, including mpox, Ebola, and rabies, in Bukavu, DRC, to identify factors associated with satisfactory overall KAP. Zoonotic diseases are a major public health concern in the DRC, where diseases such as rabies, Ebola, and Mpox pose ongoing health risks.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study was conducted from September 1 to 15, 2025, in the Bagira and Kadutu health zones of Bukavu, South Kivu Province, DRC. A total of 634 households were selected using two-stage cluster sampling.
Core methods
Data were collected using a structured questionnaire administered through KoboCollect and analyzed using Stata version 17. The questionnaire assessed knowledge, attitudes, and practices concerning Ebola, Mpox, and rabies. Knowledge was measured using 17 items, attitudes using five-point Likert-scale items, and practices using five behavioral items. Scores of at least 70% were classified as satisfactory. Descriptive statistics, group comparisons, and bivariate and multivariable logistic regression analyses were used. Nonparametric tests were used when appropriate.
Main results
Overall, 42.9% of households demonstrated satisfactory knowledge of zoonotic diseases, 63.6% had favorable attitudes, and 11.7% demonstrated adequate preventive practices. Mpox was recognized as a zoonosis by 78.2% of respondents, Ebola by 66.6%, and rabies by 39.1%. Knowledge scores were higher among livestock-farming than non-livestock-farming households (p = 0.002). In the multivariable analysis of overall KAP, university-level education (OR = 12.06; 95% CI: 1.54-94.18; p = 0.018) and access to drinking water (OR = 6.74; 95% CI: 3.91-11.60; p < 0.001) were positively associated with satisfactory overall KAP.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that households in Bukavu reported generally favorable attitudes toward zoonotic disease prevention, but satisfactory knowledge was less widespread and adequate preventive practices were uncommon. The study identified specific factors, such as university-level education and access to drinking water, that were positively associated with satisfactory overall KAP.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can help inform locally tailored prevention strategies and identify priorities for future community-based research. Interventions should help translate awareness and favorable attitudes into practical preventive behaviors through targeted risk communication and health education, alongside improvements in access to drinking water and sanitation.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design, which cannot establish causality, and by the potential for self-reporting bias. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the observational nature of the study and the lack of longitudinal data.

作者更正:猴痘病毒 M1R 和 B6R 蛋白中和抗体的生成和表征

Author Correction: Generation and characterization of neutralizing antibodies against M1R and B6R proteins of monkeypox virus
作者： Yuanyuan Qu, Wanbo Tai, Enhao Ma, Qiwei Jiang, Miao Fan, Wangcheng Xiao, Chongyu Tian, Yang Liu, Jianying Liu, Xinquan Wang
摘要中文翻译在本文最初发布的版本中,在图 7A 源数据和相关图形面板中,预防模型中 A138 + B026 组的条目在数据编译过程中被错误粘贴。本文的 HTML 和 PDF 版本中的源数据和图表已更新。为了进行比较,原始数据可作为本修正案随附的补充信息提供。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在满足了解针对猴痘病毒特定蛋白质的中和抗体的需求,因为之前的研究已将这些蛋白质确定为治疗干预的潜在目标。
设计与对象
本研究是作者的更正,解决了原始出版物中有关预防模型中 A138 + B026 组的数据呈现和源数据的错误。
核心方法
更正涉及更新文章 HTML 和 PDF 版本中的源数据和图形面板,以确保数据的准确表示,并提供原始图形作为补充信息以供参考。
主要结果
更正的主要结果是修订源数据和图形面板,以更正预防模型中 A138 + B026 组的粘贴条目,确保数据准确且格式正确。
解释与创新
作者将此次更正解释为维持数据完整性和准确性的必要调整,此次更正中没有报告新的发现。
科研与公卫意义
该校正确保了研究中提供的数据的准确性,这对于正确解释中和抗体针对猴痘病毒蛋白的有效性非常重要,支持未来的研究和治疗开发。
局限与证据强度
证据仅限于数据表述的修正,证据强度适中,因为它没有引入新的发现,但保证了先前报告数据的准确性。

用于猴痘疾病检测的 CNN-ViT 和 SE-CNN 与 grad-CAM 的混合架构

Hybrid architectures of CNN-ViT and SE-CNN with grad-CAM for monkeypox disease detection
作者： Saeed Mohsen, M. Mokhtar Zayed, Ahmed F. Ibrahim, M. Abdel-Aziz
摘要中文翻译摘要 2019 年冠状病毒病 (COVID-19) 大流行后,猴痘病毒 (MPV) 成为全球重大健康问题,导致世界卫生组织 (WHO) 宣布其为突发公共卫生事件。由于猴痘(猴痘)与麻疹和水痘等其他痘病毒相似,准确检测猴痘病毒具有挑战性。传统的诊断方法往往难以区分这些疾病,因此需要开发更先进的诊断工具。本文提出了一种使用两种高效深度学习(DL)模型来检测 猴痘 的新方法:第一个模型是卷积神经网络视觉变换器(CNN-ViT)的混合架构,第二个模型是挤压和激励卷积神经网络(SE-CNN)。这些模型用于将医学图像分为两类:猴痘和其他。所提出的模型应用于 1140 张图像的数据集,并对模型的超参数进行微调。数据增强技术用于训练图像,以最大限度地提高模型的性能。此外,应用梯度加权类激活映射(Grad-CAM)可视化技术来解释模型的决策并帮助医生进行临床决策。实验上,使用准确度、误差矩阵、受试者工作特征(ROC)曲线下面积、召回率、F1 分数和精度来衡量所提出模型的性能。CNN-ViT和SE-CNN的测试精度分别为97.80%和96.05%,而ROC曲线下的微平均面积CNN-ViT为97%,SE-CNN为99%。CNN-ViT 的加权 F1 分数、精度和召回率分别为 97.26%、97.33% 和 97.27%,而 SE-CNN 分别为 96.04%、96.13% 和 96.05%。所提出的模型提供了一种快速、准确的方法来区分 猴痘 与其他病例,为医疗保健专业人员进行 猴痘 的筛查和 诊断 提供了宝贵的工具。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
这项研究的动机是在 COVID-19 大流行后需要改进猴痘病毒 (MPV) 的检测方法,因为 MPV 已成为一个重大的全球健康问题,促使世界卫生组织 (WHO) 宣布其为突发公共卫生事件。
设计与对象
该研究提出了一种基于深度学习的新颖方法,使用医学图像检测猴痘,采用两种混合模型:CNN-ViT 和 SE-CNN,在 1140 张图像的数据集上进行训练和测试。
核心方法
这些模型针对超参数进行了微调,并使用数据增强技术进行训练以提高性能。应用梯度加权类激活映射 (Grad-CAM) 可视化技术来解释模型决策并支持临床解释。
主要结果
CNN-ViT模型的测试准确率达到97.80%,而SE-CNN模型的测试准确率达到96.05%。CNN-ViT 的 ROC 曲线下微平均面积为 97%,SE-CNN 为 99%。CNN-ViT 的加权 F1 分数、精确度和召回率分别为 97.26%、97.33% 和 97.27%,SE-CNN 分别为 96.04%、96.13% 和 96.05%。
解释与创新
作者将这些结果解释为证明了所提出的模型在准确区分猴痘与其他病例方面的有效性,强调了它们作为先进诊断工具的潜力。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现提供了一种快速、准确的检测猴痘的方法,可以帮助医疗保健专业人员进行筛查和 诊断,从而有助于更好的公共卫生应对措施。
局限与证据强度
该研究没有报告局限性,但证据基于单一数据集,不包括与其他诊断方法的比较,这可能会影响结果的普遍性。

Hybrid architectures of CNN-ViT and SE-CNN with grad-CAM for monkeypox disease detection

Authors: Saeed Mohsen, M. Mokhtar Zayed, Ahmed F. Ibrahim, M. Abdel-Aziz
Original abstract (English)
Abstract In the aftermath of the coronavirus disease 2019 (COVID-19) pandemic, the monkeypox virus (MPV) emerged as a significant global health concern, leading the World Health Organization (WHO) to declare it a public health emergency. Accurate detection of monkeypox (Mpox) is challenging due to its similarity to other pox viruses such as measles and chickenpox. Conventional diagnostic methods often struggle to distinguish between these diseases, necessitating the development of more advanced diagnostic tools. This paper presents a novel approach to detect Mpox using two efficient deep learning (DL) models: the first one is a hybrid architecture of a Convolutional Neural Network—Vision Transformer (CNN–ViT), and the second model is a Squeeze-and-Excitation Convolutional Neural Network (SE-CNN). These models are employed for the classification of medical images into two categories: Monkeypox and Others. The proposed models are applied to a dataset of 1140 images, and utilize fine-tuning for the models’ hyperparameters. Data augmentation techniques are implemented for training images to maximize the models’ performance. Additionally, a gradient-weighted class activation mapping (Grad-CAM) visualization technique is applied to explain the decision of the models and aid doctors in clinical decision-making. Experimentally, the accuracy, error matrix, area under the receiver operating characteristic (ROC) curve, Recall, F1-score, and Precision are used to measure the performance of the proposed models. The CNN–ViT and SE-CNN achieve a testing accuracy of 97.80% and 96.05%, respectively, while the micro-average area under the ROC curve is 97% for CNN–ViT and 99% for SE-CNN. The weighted F1-score, Precision, and Recall for CNN–ViT are all 97.26%, 97.33%, and 97.27%, respectively, while for SE-CNN are 96.04%, 96.13%, and 96.05%, respectively. The proposed models provide a rapid and accurate method to differentiate Mpox from other cases, offering a valuable tool for healthcare professionals in the screening and diagnosis of Mpox.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study was motivated by the need for improved detection methods for monkeypox virus (MPV) following the COVID-19 pandemic, as MPV has emerged as a significant global health concern, prompting the World Health Organization (WHO) to declare it a public health emergency.
Design and population
The study presents a novel deep learning-based approach for detecting monkeypox using medical images, employing two hybrid models: CNN–ViT and SE-CNN, which are trained and tested on a dataset of 1140 images.
Core methods
The models are fine-tuned for hyperparameters and trained using data augmentation techniques to enhance performance. A gradient-weighted class activation mapping (Grad-CAM) visualization technique is applied to explain model decisions and support clinical interpretation.
Main results
The CNN–ViT model achieved a testing accuracy of 97.80%, while the SE-CNN model achieved 96.05%. The micro-average area under the ROC curve was 97% for CNN–ViT and 99% for SE-CNN. The weighted F1-score, Precision, and Recall for CNN–ViT were all 97.26%, 97.33%, and 97.27%, respectively, and for SE-CNN were 96.04%, 96.13%, and 96.05%, respectively.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret these results as demonstrating the effectiveness of the proposed models in accurately differentiating monkeypox from other cases, highlighting their potential as advanced diagnostic tools.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings offer a rapid and accurate method for detecting monkeypox, which can aid healthcare professionals in screening and diagnosis, contributing to better public health responses.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study does not report limitations, but the evidence is based on a single dataset and does not include comparisons with other diagnostic methods, which may affect the generalizability of the results.

喀麦隆南部半农村地区 猴痘 流行的血清学和分子证据及相关危险因素

Serological and molecular evidence of an endemic presence of mpox and associated risk factors in semi-rural areas of southern Cameroon
作者： Nadine Lamare, Célestin Godwe, Guillaume Thaurignac, Sayouba Ouedraogo, Ginette Edoul, Michel Kengne, Patrice Landry Koudoum, Hermione Djuiessie, Léonie Tonfack, Thomas Atemkeng
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查喀麦隆南部半农村地区 猴痘 的流行情况及其相关危险因素,满足了解这些地区病毒传播和 猴痘 动态的需要。
设计与对象
2025年5月至8月在喀麦隆南部进行了一项横断面调查和案例调查,涉及1072人(523名女性和549名男性),中位年龄为30岁。
核心方法
对样本进行 猴痘 抗体和 DNA 筛查,并对 PCR 阳性样本进行测序以确定病毒的进化枝。
主要结果
1072份样本中,122份(11.4%)猴痘抗体阳性,病例调查中仅指示病例PCR阳性,经测序证实为猴痘 clade Ia。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,猴痘 在所研究的喀麦隆南部半农村地区流行,突出表明需要有针对性的 猴痘 和干预策略。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果为半农村地区 猴痘 的流行性质提供了重要见解,可为 猴痘 的公共卫生策略、预防和控制提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性包括样本量相对较小以及自我报告的风险因素存在潜在偏差,这可能会影响研究结果的普遍性。由于这些限制,证据的强度中等。

Serological and molecular evidence of an endemic presence of mpox and associated risk factors in semi-rural areas of southern Cameroon

Authors: Nadine Lamare, Célestin Godwe, Guillaume Thaurignac, Sayouba Ouedraogo, Ginette Edoul, Michel Kengne, Patrice Landry Koudoum, Hermione Djuiessie, Léonie Tonfack, Thomas Atemkeng
Original abstract (English)
The mpox epidemic in Africa is maintained by zoonotic spill-over events and sustained human-to-human transmissions. Our objective in this work was to conduct serological and molecular surveys on the circulation of mpox in Southern Cameroon and associated risk factors. To that end, we conducted cross-sectional surveys and a case investigation between May and August 2025. We screened samples for mpox antibodies and DNA and sequenced PCR-positive samples. Overall, we included 1072 persons (523 women and 549 men) with a median age of 30 years. A total of 122/1072 (11.4%) samples were mpox antibody positive. Seroprevalence was higher in samples collected in August than in May 2025. Risk factors significantly associated with positivity to mpox antibodies were age above 66 years, being widowed, having been bitten at least once by a wild animal and having contact with wildlife. Of the five samples from case investigation, only the index case was PCR-positive (H, 52 years old), confirmed as mpox clade Ia by sequencing (group I). In serology, 2 of the 5 samples of the household (index case and his 13-year-old son) were mpox antibody positive. This pilot work suggests endemic circulation of mpox in the studied semi-rural districts of southern Cameroon.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the endemic presence of mpox and its associated risk factors in semi-rural areas of southern Cameroon, addressing the need to understand the virus's circulation and transmission dynamics in these regions.
Design and population
A cross-sectional survey and case investigation were conducted between May and August 2025 in Southern Cameroon, involving 1072 persons (523 women and 549 men) with a median age of 30 years.
Core methods
Samples were screened for mpox antibodies and DNA, and PCR-positive samples were sequenced to determine the clade of the virus.
Main results
Out of 1072 samples, 122 (11.4%) were mpox antibody positive, and in the case investigation, only the index case was PCR-positive, confirmed as mpox clade Ia by sequencing.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that mpox is circulating endemically in the studied semi-rural districts of southern Cameroon, highlighting the need for targeted surveillance and intervention strategies.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results provide critical insights into the endemic nature of mpox in semi-rural areas, which can inform public health strategies for surveillance, prevention, and control.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's limitations include a relatively small sample size and potential biases in self-reported risk factors, which may affect the generalizability of the findings. The evidence is moderate in strength due to these constraints.

猴痘生殖器感染的管理 (猴痘),刚果民主共和国,2025 年 4 月至 6 月

Management of Genital Involvement in Monkeypox (MPOX), DRC, April–June 2025
作者： Dr. Moussa Issa Lende
摘要中文翻译简介:猴痘 (猴痘) 目前是刚果民主共和国 (DRC) 的一个主要公共卫生问题,该疾病仍然流行。尽管 猴痘 的全身皮肤表现在科学文献中有相对详细的记录,但生殖器受累的描述仍然不充分,尽管其具有显着的临床、营养、心理社会和社区相关影响。本研究旨在描述桑库鲁省受 猴痘 影响、出现生殖器病变的患者的临床、营养和社会心理特征。方法和材料:这是 2025 年 4 月至 6 月在刚果民主共和国桑库鲁省进行的描述性横断面病例系列。从 8 名确诊为 猴痘 生殖器受累的患者收集了社会人口学、临床、营养、治疗和社会心理数据。患者是通过详尽的方法从洛贾猴痘 治疗 中心 (CTM) 收治的病例中选择的。对收集到的信息进行分析,以描述疾病的临床表现、可能的 传播 途径以及综合患者管理的方式。与身体其他部位的皮肤病变相比,生殖器病变的愈合速度明显较慢,平均愈合时间为 21 ± 3.2 天,而周围病变的平均愈合时间为 13 ± 2.8 天 (p < 0.05)。62.5% 的患者出现继发细菌重复感染。此外,两名患者 (25%) 出现严重营养不良 (BMI < 16 kg/m2),这与明显更长的愈合时间相关,平均为 24 ± 2 天,而营养状况正常的患者为 19 ± 3 天 (p = 0.04)。传播的可能模式包括性传播(37.5%)、家庭内传播(25%)、社交聚会期间的社区传播(25%)和直系配偶传播(12.5%)。结合医疗 治疗、营养支持和心理社会护理的整体管理方法使 87.5% 的患者完全康复,而在出院后随访期间观察到 75% 的患者坚持预防措施。结论:猴痘 的生殖器受累是桑库鲁省的一种重要临床表现,其特点是愈合较慢且局部并发症的风险增加。这些发现强调了将医疗护理、营养支持、社会心理干预和社区参与结合起来的综合管理的必要性。将这些方法纳入公共卫生策略可能有助于改善患者的临床预后、减少疾病并促进受影响个体重新融入社会和家庭。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
猴痘 (猴痘) 目前是刚果民主共和国 (DRC) 的一个主要公共卫生问题,该疾病仍然流行。尽管 猴痘 的全身皮肤表现在科学文献中有相对详细的记录,但生殖器受累的描述仍然不充分,尽管其具有显着的临床、营养、心理社会和社区相关影响。
设计与对象
这项研究于 2025 年 4 月至 6 月在刚果民主共和国,特别是桑库鲁省进行。通过详尽的方法从洛贾猴痘 治疗 中心 (CTM) 收治的病例中选择患者。
核心方法
这是一个描述性的横截面案例系列。从 8 名确诊 猴痘 生殖器受累的患者中收集了社会人口学、临床、营养、治疗和心理社会数据。对收集到的信息进行分析,以描述疾病的临床表现、可能的 传播 途径以及综合患者管理的方式。
主要结果
与身体其他部位的皮肤病变相比,生殖器病变的愈合速度明显较慢,平均愈合时间为 21 ± 3.2 天,而周围病变的平均愈合时间为 13 ± 2.8 天 (p < 0.05)。此外,两名患者 (25%) 出现严重营养不良 (BMI < 16 kg/m2),这与明显更长的愈合时间相关,平均为 24 ± 2 天,而营养状况正常的患者为 19 ± 3 天 (p = 0.04)。
解释与创新
在桑库鲁省,猴痘 的生殖器受累是一种重要的临床表现,其特点是愈合速度较慢,局部并发症的风险增加。这些发现强调了将医疗护理、营养支持、社会心理干预和社区参与结合起来的综合管理的必要性。
科研与公卫意义
结合医疗 治疗、营养支持和心理社会护理的整体管理方法使 87.5% 的患者完全康复。将这些方法纳入公共卫生策略可能有助于改善患者的临床预后、减少疾病并促进受影响个体重新融入社会和家庭。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到样本量小(八名患者)的限制,并且由于选择病例时采用了详尽的方法而可能出现选择偏倚。由于范围和样本量有限,证据强度中等。

Management of Genital Involvement in Monkeypox (MPOX), DRC, April–June 2025

Authors: Dr. Moussa Issa Lende
Original abstract (English)
Introduction: Monkeypox (MPOX) currently constitutes a major public health concern in the Democratic Republic of Congo (DRC), where the disease remains endemic. Although the generalized cutaneous manifestations of MPOX are relatively well documented in the scientific literature, genital involvement remains insufficiently described, despite its significant clinical, nutritional, psychosocial, and community-related implications. This study aims to describe the clinical, nutritional, and psychosocial characteristics of patients affected by MPOX presenting genital lesions in the Sankuru province. Methods and Materials: This is a descriptive cross-sectional case series conducted in the Democratic Republic of Congo, in the Sankuru province, between April and June 2025. Sociodemographic, clinical, nutritional, therapeutic, and psychosocial data were collected from eight patients with confirmed genital involvement of MPOX. Patients were selected through an exhaustive approach among cases admitted to the Monkeypox Treatment Centers (CTM) in Lodja. The collected information was analyzed to describe the clinical presentation of the disease, the probable transmission routes, and the modalities of integrated patient management. Genital lesions exhibited significantly slower healing compared to cutaneous lesions located on other body areas, with a mean healing time of 21 ± 3.2 days versus 13 ± 2.8 days for peripheral lesions (p < 0.05). Secondary bacterial superinfections were observed in 62.5% of patients. Additionally, two patients (25%) presented severe malnutrition (BMI < 16 kg/m2), which was associated with a significantly longer healing duration, averaging 24 ± 2 days compared to 19 ± 3 days in patients with normal nutritional status (p = 0.04). The probable modes of transmission included sexual transmission (37.5%), intrafamilial transmission (25%), community transmission during social gatherings (25%), and direct spousal transmission (12.5%). A holistic management approach combining medical treatment, nutritional support, and psychosocial care resulted in complete recovery in 87.5% of patients, while adherence to preventive measures was observed in 75% of patients during post-hospital follow-up. Conclusion: Genital involvement in MPOX represents a significant clinical manifestation in the Sankuru province, characterized by slower healing and an increased risk of local complications. These findings highlight the necessity of integrated management combining medical care, nutritional support, psychosocial interventions, and community engagement. Integrating these approaches into public health strategies may contribute to improving the clinical prognosis of patients, reducing disease transmission, and promoting the social and familial reintegration of affected individuals.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Monkeypox (MPOX) currently constitutes a major public health concern in the Democratic Republic of Congo (DRC), where the disease remains endemic. Although the generalized cutaneous manifestations of MPOX are relatively well documented in the scientific literature, genital involvement remains insufficiently described, despite its significant clinical, nutritional, psychosocial, and community-related implications.

This study was conducted in the Democratic Republic of Congo, specifically in the Sankuru province, between April and June 2025. Patients were selected through an exhaustive approach among cases admitted to the Monkeypox Treatment Centers (CTM) in Lodja.

This is a descriptive cross-sectional case series. Sociodemographic, clinical, nutritional, therapeutic, and psychosocial data were collected from eight patients with confirmed genital involvement of MPOX. The collected information was analyzed to describe the clinical presentation of the disease, the probable transmission routes, and the modalities of integrated patient management.
Main results
Genital lesions exhibited significantly slower healing compared to cutaneous lesions located on other body areas, with a mean healing time of 21 ± 3.2 days versus 13 ± 2.8 days for peripheral lesions (p < 0.05). Additionally, two patients (25%) presented severe malnutrition (BMI < 16 kg/m2), which was associated with a significantly longer healing duration, averaging 24 ± 2 days compared to 19 ± 3 days in patients with normal nutritional status (p = 0.04).
Interpretation and novelty
Genital involvement in MPOX represents a significant clinical manifestation in the Sankuru province, characterized by slower healing and an increased risk of local complications. These findings highlight the necessity of integrated management combining medical care, nutritional support, psychosocial interventions, and community engagement.
Scientific and public-health significance
A holistic management approach combining medical treatment, nutritional support, and psychosocial care resulted in complete recovery in 87.5% of patients. Integrating these approaches into public health strategies may contribute to improving the clinical prognosis of patients, reducing disease transmission, and promoting the social and familial reintegration of affected individuals.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its small sample size (eight patients) and the potential for selection bias due to the exhaustive approach used to select cases. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the limited scope and sample size.

Hubungan Pengetahuauan dengan Sikap Kader Kesehatan Mengenai Penyakit 猴痘 井里汶哥打

Hubungan Pengetahuan dengan Sikap Kader Kesehatan Mengenai Penyakit Mpox di Kota Cirebon
作者： Via Aprista Ayu, Witri Pratiwi, Mustika Weni
摘要中文翻译摘要 猴痘(猴痘)是由正痘病毒引起的人畜共患疾病。自2022年5月以来,猴痘病例在包括印度尼西亚在内的各国急剧增加。良好的知识有望形成积极的态度来面对这种疾病的威胁。确定井里汶市卫生干部对 猴痘 疾病的知识和态度之间的关系。本研究采用横断面设计和观察分析方法。数据收集于 2025 年 5 月至 6 月在井里汶市的 5 个卫生中心进行。受访者包括通过整群随机抽样抽取的 100 名卫生干部。使用结构化问卷收集数据并使用斯皮尔曼检验进行分析。结果显示,53%的干部知识水平低,30%的干部知识水平中等,17%的干部知识水平高。共有69%的受访者对猴痘预防持消极态度,而31%的受访者持积极态度。卫生干部的知识和态度之间存在显着且强的相关性(p0.001;rho=0.773)。井里汶市大部分卫生干部对猴痘的预防认知度较低,态度消极。知识水平和态度之间存在着密切而显着的关系。需要通过培训和健康教育对干部进行能力建设,以支持 猴痘 预防工作。关键词:猴痘、知识、态度、卫生干部、井里汶。摘要 猴痘(cacar monyet)adalah penyakit 人兽共患病 yang disebabkan oleh 病毒正痘。Sejak Mei 2022,kasus 猴痘 meningkat tajam di berbagai negara termasuk Indonesia。请注意以下事项: Menetahui hubungan antara pengetahuan dengan sikap kader kesehatan mengenai penyakit 猴痘 di Kota Cirebon。Penelitian ini menggunakan desain cross-section dengan pendekatan analitik observasional. 2025 年 5 月 Kota Cirebon 的 Puskesmas 的数据显示为 Mei-Juni。响应 terdiri dari 100 kader kesehatan yang dialilih melalui 整群随机抽样。数据可以通过 Uji Spearman 的结构和分析来确定。Hasil penelitian menunjukkan 53% kader memiliki pengetahuan rendah、30% pengetahuan sedang、dan 17% pengetahua tinggi。Sebanyak 69% 的受访者对 猴痘 表示否定,31% 的受访者表示积极。Terdapat hubungan yangsignifikan dan kuat antara pengetahuan dengan sikap kader kesehatan (p0,001;rho=0,773)。Sebagian besar kader kesehatan di Kota Cirebon memiliki pengetahuan yang rendah dan sikap negatif terhadap pencegahan 猴痘。Terdapat hubungan yang kuat dansignifikan antara tingkat pengetahuan dengan sikap。请注意以下事项:猴痘 和教育。Kata Kunci:猴痘、Pengetahuan、Sikap、Kader Kesehatan、井里汶。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在调查井里汶市卫生干部对猴痘病的知识与态度的关系。猴痘是由正痘病毒引起的人畜共患疾病,自2022年5月以来在包括印度尼西亚在内的多个国家病例急剧增加。
设计与对象
本研究采用横断面设计和观察分析方法。数据收集于2025年5月至6月在井里汶市5个卫生中心进行,涉及通过整群随机抽样抽取的100名卫生干部。
核心方法
使用结构化调查问卷收集数据,并使用斯皮尔曼测试进行分析,以评估知识和态度之间的关系。
主要结果
结果显示,53%的干部知识水平低,30%的干部知识水平中等,17%的干部知识水平高。共有69%的受访者对猴痘预防持消极态度,而31%的受访者持积极态度。知识水平和态度之间存在显着且强烈的关系(p0.001;rho=0.773)。
解释与创新
作者得出的结论是,卫生干部对 猴痘 预防的知识和态度之间存在密切且显着的关系。这一发现凸显了了解影响卫生干部态度的因素的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,通过培训和教育提高卫生干部的知识可以积极影响他们的态度,从而支持社区中更有效的 猴痘 预防工作。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到横截面设计的限制,无法确定因果关系。然而,由于发现了显着且强的相关性(p0.001;rho=0.773),因此证据是强有力的,并且使用整群随机抽样确保了样本的代表性。

Hubungan Pengetahuan dengan Sikap Kader Kesehatan Mengenai Penyakit Mpox di Kota Cirebon

Authors: Via Aprista Ayu, Witri Pratiwi, Mustika Weni
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Mpox (monkey pox) is a zoonotic disease caused by the orthopox virus. Since May 2022, Mpox cases have increased sharply in various countries including Indonesia. Good knowledge is expected to form a positive attitude in facing the threat of this disease. To determine the relationship between knowledge and attitude of health cadres regarding Mpox disease in Cirebon City. This study used a cross-sectional design with an observational analytic approach. Data collection was conducted in May-June 2025 at 5 health centers in Cirebon City. Respondents consisted of 100 health cadres selected through cluster random sampling. Data were collected using a structured questionnaire and analyzed with the Spearman test. The results showed that 53% of cadres had low knowledge, 30% moderate knowledge, and 17% high knowledge. A total of 69% of respondents had a negative attitude towards Mpox prevention, while 31% had a positive attitude. There was a significant and strong relationship between knowledge and attitude of health cadres (p0.001; rho=0.773). Most health cadres in Cirebon City have low knowledge and negative attitudes towards Mpox prevention. There is a strong and significant relationship between knowledge level and attitude. Capacity building of cadres through training and health education is needed to support Mpox prevention efforts. Keywords: Mpox, Knowledge, Attitude, Health Cadres, Cirebon. ABSTRAK Mpox (cacar monyet) adalah penyakit zoonosis yang disebabkan oleh virus orthopox. Sejak Mei 2022, kasus Mpox meningkat tajam di berbagai negara termasuk Indonesia. Pengetahuan yang baik diharapkan dapat membentuk sikap positif dalam menghadapi ancaman penyakit ini. Mengetahui hubungan antara pengetahuan dengan sikap kader kesehatan mengenai penyakit Mpox di Kota Cirebon. Penelitian ini menggunakan desain cross-sectional dengan pendekatan analitik observasional. Pengambilan data dilakukan pada Mei-Juni 2025 di 5 Puskesmas di Kota Cirebon. Responden terdiri dari 100 kader kesehatan yang dipilih melalui cluster random sampling. Data dikumpulkan menggunakan kuesioner terstruktur dan dianalisis dengan uji Spearman. Hasil penelitian menunjukkan 53% kader memiliki pengetahuan rendah, 30% pengetahuan sedang, dan 17% pengetahuan tinggi. Sebanyak 69% responden memiliki sikap negatif terhadap pencegahan Mpox, sementara 31% memiliki sikap positif. Terdapat hubungan yang signifikan dan kuat antara pengetahuan dengan sikap kader kesehatan (p0,001; rho=0,773). Sebagian besar kader kesehatan di Kota Cirebon memiliki pengetahuan yang rendah dan sikap negatif terhadap pencegahan Mpox. Terdapat hubungan yang kuat dan signifikan antara tingkat pengetahuan dengan sikap. Peningkatan kapasitas kader melalui pelatihan dan edukasi kesehatan sangat diperlukan untuk mendukung upaya pencegahan Mpox. Kata Kunci: Mpox, Pengetahuan, Sikap, Kader Kesehatan, Cirebon.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the relationship between knowledge and attitude of health cadres regarding Mpox disease in Cirebon City. Mpox is a zoonotic disease caused by the orthopox virus, and there has been a sharp increase in cases since May 2022 in various countries including Indonesia.
Design and population
This study used a cross-sectional design with an observational analytic approach. Data collection was conducted in May-June 2025 at 5 health centers in Cirebon City, involving 100 health cadres selected through cluster random sampling.
Core methods
Data were collected using a structured questionnaire and analyzed with the Spearman test to assess the relationship between knowledge and attitude.
Main results
The results showed that 53% of cadres had low knowledge, 30% moderate knowledge, and 17% high knowledge. A total of 69% of respondents had a negative attitude towards Mpox prevention, while 31% had a positive attitude. There was a significant and strong relationship between knowledge level and attitude (p0.001; rho=0.773).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that there is a strong and significant relationship between knowledge and attitude of health cadres towards Mpox prevention. This finding highlights the importance of understanding the factors influencing health cadres' attitudes.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that improving the knowledge of health cadres through training and education could positively influence their attitudes, thereby supporting more effective Mpox prevention efforts in the community.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design, which cannot establish causality. However, the evidence is strong due to the significant and strong correlation found (p0.001; rho=0.773), and the use of cluster random sampling ensures a representative sample.

HECA-YOLOv11:一种高精度混合注意力网络,用于实时猴痘皮肤病变检测并具有可解释性

HECA-YOLOv11: A High-Accuracy Hybrid Attention Network for Real-Time Monkeypox Skin Lesion Detection with Interpretability
作者： Xianghong Deng, Chonlatee Photong
摘要中文翻译简介:全球 暴发 猴痘风险需要高效的诊断工具。目前的方法速度缓慢,并且难以区分相似的皮肤病变,而深度学习模型缺乏多类分类的准确性和可解释性。本研究旨在开发一种高性能、实时筛查工具,用于早期检测猴痘和类似病症。方法:本研究提出了一种增强的架构,将高效通道注意力(HECA)机制纳入 YOLOv11 中,以改进特征选择。HECA 对输入特征图执行全局平均池化 (GAP) 和最大平均池化 (MAP)。该研究利用空间变换和像素域增强,建立并增强了包含猴痘、麻疹、水痘和正常皮肤图像的四类数据集。梯度加权类激活映射 (Grad-CAM) 生成用于病变定位可视化的热图,而基于 PyQt5 的交互式诊断界面则简化了临床工作流程。结果:实验结果表明,与测试集上的现有方法(EfficientNet-v2-l、GoogLeNet、ResNet50、VGG19、MobileNetv2、DenseNet201 和 YOLOv11s)相比,所提出的模型具有优越的性能,具有增强的检测速度、准确度(98.9%)、精确度(99%)、召回率(99.3%)和 F1 分数(99.1%)。在所有评估指标中,HECA-YOLOv11 的性能均优于 SE-YOLOv11 和 ECA-YOLOv11 超过 5.1%。热图分析证实了该模型对病变区域的关注,与医学推理相一致。讨论:集成的注意力机制和可解释性技术有效地解决了自动皮肤病变检测的关键限制。高性能和低延迟标志着可靠临床决策支持的重大进步,对早期 暴发 遏制具有重要意义。结论:HECA-YOLOv11框架为早期猴痘筛查提供了高度准确和高效的工具。其标准化界面在加速公共卫生应对和改善新发传染病的全球控制方面具有巨大潜力。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了公共卫生对快速、准确的诊断工具的迫切需求,以减轻 猴痘 的全球 暴发 风险,强调现有方法速度缓慢,而且深度学习模型缺乏多类皮肤病变分类的准确性和可解释性。
设计与对象
作者开发并评估了一个深度学习框架 (HECA-YOLOv11),旨在使用四类图像数据集实时筛查 猴痘 和类似的皮肤病变。
核心方法
该架构将高效通道注意力(HECA)机制集成到YOLOv11中,将全局平均池化和最大平均池化应用于输入特征图,并使用空间变换和像素域增强来增强包含猴痘、麻疹、水痘和正常皮肤图像的数据集。
主要结果
在测试集上,HECA-YOLOv11 实现了 98.9% 的准确率、99% 的精确率、99.3% 的召回率和 99.1% 的 F1 分数,优于 EfficientNet-v2-l、GoogLeNet、ResNet50、VGG19、MobileNetv2、DenseNet201、YOLOv11s、SE-YOLOv11 和 ECA-YOLOv11所有指标均为 5.1%。
解释与创新
作者将集成注意力机制和可解释性技术解释为有效解决自动皮肤病变检测的关键限制,标志着早期 猴痘 筛查的可靠临床决策支持的重大进步。
科研与公卫意义
HECA-YOLOv11 的标准化界面和高性能表明有可能通过早期检测加速公共卫生反应并改善对新发传染病的全球控制。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据未报告数据集规模、地理或人群范围、外部验证或前瞻性临床评估,限制了结论的普遍性,且证据强度为中等。

HECA-YOLOv11: A High-Accuracy Hybrid Attention Network for Real-Time Monkeypox Skin Lesion Detection with Interpretability

Authors: Xianghong Deng, Chonlatee Photong
Original abstract (English)
Introduction: The global outbreak risk of monkeypox necessitates efficient diagnostic tools. Current methods are slow and struggle to differentiate similar skin lesions, while deep learning models lack both accuracy and interpretability for multi-class classification. This study aims to develop a high-performance, real-time screening tool for early detection of monkeypox and similar conditions. Methods: This study proposes an enhanced architecture that incorporates the Highly Efficient Channel Attention (HECA) mechanism into YOLOv11 to improve feature selection. HECA performs Global Average Pooling (GAP) and Max Average Pooling (MAP) on input feature maps. Using spatial transformations and pixel-domain enhancements, the research establishes and augments a four-class dataset containing monkeypox, measles, chickenpox, and normal skin images. Gradient-Weighted Class Activation Mapping (Grad-CAM) generates heatmaps for lesion localization visualization, while a PyQt5-based interactive diagnostic interface streamlines clinical workflows. Results: Experimental results demonstrate the proposed model’s superior performance compared to existing approaches (EfficientNet-v2-l, GoogLeNet, ResNet50, VGG19, MobileNetv2, DenseNet201, and YOLOv11s) on the test set, with enhanced detection speed, accuracy (98.9%), precision (99%), recall (99.3%), and F1-score (99.1%). HECA-YOLOv11 outperforms SE-YOLOv11 and ECA-YOLOv11 by over 5.1% across all evaluation metrics. Heatmap analysis confirms the model’s focus on lesion regions, aligning with medical reasoning. Discussion: The integrated attention mechanism and interpretability techniques effectively address key limitations in automated skin lesion detection. The high performance and low latency signify a major advancement towards reliable clinical decision support, with important implications for early outbreak containment. Conclusion: The HECA-YOLOv11 framework provides a highly accurate and efficient tool for early monkeypox screening. Its standardized interface holds significant potential to accelerate public health responses and improve global control of emerging infectious diseases.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the urgent public-health need for rapid, accurate diagnostic tools to mitigate the global outbreak risk of mpox, highlighting that existing methods are slow and deep learning models lack both accuracy and interpretability for multi-class skin lesion classification.
Design and population
The authors developed and evaluated a deep learning framework (HECA-YOLOv11) designed for real-time screening of mpox and similar skin lesions using a four-class image dataset.
Core methods
The proposed architecture integrates a Highly Efficient Channel Attention (HECA) mechanism into YOLOv11, applying Global Average Pooling and Max Average Pooling to input feature maps, and augments a dataset containing monkeypox, measles, chickenpox, and normal skin images using spatial transformations and pixel-domain enhancements.
Main results
On the test set, HECA-YOLOv11 achieved 98.9% accuracy, 99% precision, 99.3% recall, and 99.1% F1-score, outperforming EfficientNet-v2-l, GoogLeNet, ResNet50, VGG19, MobileNetv2, DenseNet201, YOLOv11s, SE-YOLOv11, and ECA-YOLOv11 by over 5.1% across all metrics.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the integrated attention mechanism and interpretability techniques as effectively addressing key limitations in automated skin lesion detection, marking a major advancement toward reliable clinical decision support for early mpox screening.
Scientific and public-health significance
The standardized interface and high performance of HECA-YOLOv11 suggest potential to accelerate public health responses and improve global control of emerging infectious diseases through early detection.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report dataset size, geographic or demographic scope, external validation, or prospective clinical evaluation, limiting generalisability and leaving the strength of evidence moderate.

土耳其人群中 猴痘 知识和预防行为措施的开发和心理测量验证

Development and psychometric validation of Mpox knowledge and preventive behavior measures in the Turkish population
作者： Ayşe Çal, Akgün Yeşiltepe, Funda Kocaay, Akgün Yeşi̇ltepe
摘要中文翻译摘要 本研究旨在为土耳其人群开发 猴痘 知识测试 (M-KT) 和 猴痘 预防行为量表 (M-PBS),并检查他们的心理测量特性。这项方法学研究包括 449 名参与者。CFA 结果表明模型拟合可接受(RMSEA = 0.076,CFI = 0.952,IFI = 0.952,χ2/df = 2.293)。其他研究结果为 M-PBS 的收敛和判别有效性提供了支持。对于M-KT,评估了项目难度、项目区分度和内部一致性,而评估了M-PBS 的有效性和可靠性。对于 M-KT,检查了内容效度、项目难度、项目辨别力和内部一致性 (KR-20)。对于 M-PBS,数据集被随机分为两个子样本。使用第一个子样本进行探索性因素分析 (EFA),然后对第二个子样本进行验证性因素分析 (CFA)。还评估了内部一致性以及收敛效度和判别效度。M-KT 显示出令人满意的项目特征和较高的内部一致性(KR-20 = 0.915)。同样,M-PBS 表现出较高的内部一致性(Cronbach’s α = 0.92)。M-PBS 的内容效度足够(S-CVI/Ave = 0.91)。EFA 支持 13 项三因素结构(KMO = 0.919;Bartlett 检验,χ2 = 2047.040,p < 0.001),这解释了总方差的 62.581%。M-KT 和 M-PBS 表现出令人满意的心理测量特性,并显示出有效性和可靠性的证据。因此,这些工具可能有助于评估土耳其人口中的 猴痘 相关知识和预防行为。其他人正在查看类似内容 资金 没有资金。道德声明 竞争利益 作者声明不存在竞争...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在为土耳其人群开发 猴痘 知识测试 (M-KT) 和 猴痘 预防行为量表 (M-PBS) 并检查他们的心理测量特性。这解决了对经过验证的工具来评估特定人群中 猴痘 相关知识和预防行为的需求。
设计与对象
一项涉及 449 名参与者的方法学研究旨在开发和验证 M-KT 和 M-PBS。
核心方法
M-KT 评估内容有效性、项目难度、项目区分度和内部一致性 (KR-20),而 M-PBS 使用探索性和验证性因素分析评估有效性和可靠性。
主要结果
M-KT 显示出令人满意的项目特征和较高的内部一致性(KR-20 = 0.915),M-PBS 显示出较高的内部一致性(Cronbach’s α = 0.92)。
解释与创新
M-KT 和 M-PBS 表现出令人满意的心理测量特性,并提供了有效性和可靠性的证据,表明它们可用于评估土耳其人群中的 猴痘 相关知识和预防行为。
科研与公卫意义
这些工具对于评估 猴痘 相关知识和预防行为可能很有价值,有助于公共卫生 监测 和干预策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到样本量和针对的特定人群的限制,这可能会影响普遍性。由于这些限制,证据强度中等。

Development and psychometric validation of Mpox knowledge and preventive behavior measures in the Turkish population

Authors: Ayşe Çal, Akgün Yeşiltepe, Funda Kocaay, Akgün Yeşi̇ltepe
Original abstract (English)
Abstract This study aimed to develop the Mpox Knowledge Test (M-KT) and the Mpox Preventive Behavior Scale (M-PBS) for the Turkish population and to examine their psychometric properties. This methodological study included 449 participants. The CFA results indicated an acceptable model fit (RMSEA = 0.076, CFI = 0.952, IFI = 0.952, χ2/df = 2.293). Additional findings provided support for the convergent and discriminant validity of the M-PBS. For the M-KT, item difficulty, item discrimination, and internal consistency were evaluated, whereas the validity and reliability of the M-PBS were assessed. For the M-KT, content validity, item difficulty, item discrimination, and internal consistency (KR-20) were examined. For the M-PBS, the dataset was randomly divided into two subsamples. Exploratory factor analysis (EFA) was performed using the first subsample, followed by confirmatory factor analysis (CFA) with the second. Internal consistency, as well as convergent and discriminant validity, was also evaluated. The M-KT showed satisfactory item characteristics and high internal consistency (KR-20 = 0.915). Similarly, the M-PBS demonstrated high internal consistency (Cronbach’s α = 0.92). Content validity of the M-PBS was adequate (S-CVI/Ave = 0.91). The EFA supported a 13-item, three-factor structure (KMO = 0.919; Bartlett’s test, χ2 = 2047.040, p < 0.001), which explained 62.581% of the total variance. The M-KT and M-PBS demonstrated satisfactory psychometric properties and showed evidence of validity and reliability. These instruments may therefore be useful for assessing mpox-related knowledge and preventive behaviors among the Turkish population. Similar content being viewed by others Funding No funding. View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to develop the Mpox Knowledge Test (M-KT) and the Mpox Preventive Behavior Scale (M-PBS) for the Turkish population and to examine their psychometric properties. This addressed the need for validated tools to assess mpox-related knowledge and preventive behaviors in a specific population.
Design and population
A methodological study involving 449 participants was conducted to develop and validate the M-KT and M-PBS.
Core methods
The M-KT was evaluated for content validity, item difficulty, item discrimination, and internal consistency (KR-20), while the M-PBS was assessed for validity and reliability using exploratory and confirmatory factor analyses.
Main results
The M-KT showed satisfactory item characteristics and high internal consistency (KR-20 = 0.915), and the M-PBS demonstrated high internal consistency (Cronbach’s α = 0.92).
Interpretation and novelty
The M-KT and M-PBS demonstrated satisfactory psychometric properties and provided evidence of validity and reliability, suggesting their usefulness for assessing mpox-related knowledge and preventive behaviors among the Turkish population.
Scientific and public-health significance
These instruments may be valuable for assessing mpox-related knowledge and preventive behaviors, contributing to public health surveillance and intervention strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its sample size and the specific population it targeted, which may affect generalizability. The evidence strength is moderate due to these limitations.

针对 MPXV 包膜病毒颗粒上不同表位的抗体可提供附加且完整的保护。

Antibodies to distinct epitopes on MPXV enveloped virion provide additive and complete protection.
作者： Ding Zhang, Raianna Farhat Fantin, Xueyong Zhu, Ziqi Feng, Seok-Chan Park, Gabriel Laghlali, Jordan J. Clark, Guilherme de Moraes Nobrega, Angelica C Kottkamp, Marie I. Samanovic
摘要中文翻译猴痘病毒 (MPXV) 分支的持续 进化 能够持续人与人之间的 进化 造成全球健康风险不断升级,但目前尚无针对痘病毒感染的有效治疗方法。在这里,我们研究了一组源自抗体库的人类单克隆抗体 (mAb),其靶向位于细胞外有膜病毒粒子 (EV) 上的 A35 和 B6 抗原。四种高亲和力 mAb 靶向 A35 二聚体的中心凹槽,而一种靶向 A35 突出环上先前未识别的表位。其他三种 mAb 可识别多域 B6 抗原上先前未定位的不同表位。这些表位在大多数 正痘病毒 中都是保守的。抗 A35 和抗 B6 抗体的组合在致死性 VACV 小鼠模型中提供了针对发病和 死亡率 的附加且完整的保护,挑战了之前的假设,即需要同时针对 EV 和成熟病毒颗粒 (MV) 形式才能实现全面保护。这些研究揭示了针对新兴 MPXV 以及其他 正痘病毒 威胁的下一代疗法和 疫苗 的关键信息。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
猴痘病毒 (MPXV) 分支的持续 进化 能够持续人与人之间的 进化 造成全球健康风险不断升级,但目前尚无针对痘病毒感染的有效治疗方法。
设计与对象
在这里,我们研究了一组源自抗体库的人类单克隆抗体 (mAb),其靶向位于细胞外有膜病毒粒子 (EV) 上的 A35 和 B6 抗原。
核心方法
四种高亲和力 mAb 靶向 A35 二聚体的中心凹槽,而一种靶向 A35 突出环上先前未识别的表位。
主要结果
其他三种 mAb 可识别多域 B6 抗原上先前未定位的不同表位。
解释与创新
这些表位在大多数 正痘病毒 中都是保守的。
科研与公卫意义
抗 A35 和抗 B6 抗体的组合在致死性 VACV 小鼠模型中提供了针对发病和 死亡率 的附加且完整的保护,挑战了之前的假设,即需要同时针对 EV 和成熟病毒颗粒 (MV) 形式才能实现全面保护。
局限与证据强度
这些研究揭示了针对新兴 MPXV 以及其他 正痘病毒 威胁的下一代疗法和 疫苗 的关键信息。

Antibodies to distinct epitopes on MPXV enveloped virion provide additive and complete protection.

Authors: Ding Zhang, Raianna Farhat Fantin, Xueyong Zhu, Ziqi Feng, Seok-Chan Park, Gabriel Laghlali, Jordan J. Clark, Guilherme de Moraes Nobrega, Angelica C Kottkamp, Marie I. Samanovic
Original abstract (English)
The continuing evolution of monkeypox virus (MPXV) clades capable of sustained human-to-human transmission poses an escalating global health risk, yet no effective therapy exists for poxvirus infection. Here, we investigate a panel of human monoclonal antibodies (mAbs), derived from an antibody library, which targets A35 and B6 antigens located on the extracellular enveloped virion (EV). Four high-affinity mAbs target the central groove of the A35 dimer, whereas one targets a previously unidentified epitope on the A35 protruding loop. Three other mAbs recognize distinct, previously unmapped epitopes on the multidomain B6 antigen. Those epitopes are conserved across most orthopoxviruses. A combination of an anti-A35 and an anti-B6 antibody provides additive and complete protection from both morbidity and mortality in a lethal VACV mouse model, challenging the previous assumption that targeting both the EV and mature virion (MV) forms is required for full protection. These studies uncover critical information for next-generation therapeutics and vaccines against emerging MPXV as well as other Orthopoxvirus threats.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The continuing evolution of monkeypox virus (MPXV) clades capable of sustained human-to-human transmission poses an escalating global health risk, yet no effective therapy exists for poxvirus infection.
Design and population

Core methods

Main results
Three other mAbs recognize distinct, previously unmapped epitopes on the multidomain B6 antigen.
Interpretation and novelty
Those epitopes are conserved across most orthopoxviruses.
Scientific and public-health significance

Afanc:病原体 基因组监测 的分类反卷积和概率谱系分配

Afanc: taxonomic deconvolution and probabilistic lineage assignment for pathogen genomic surveillance
作者： Arthur V. Morris, Anna Price, Thomas R. Connor
摘要中文翻译摘要背景:可靠的病原体 基因组监测 需要广泛的分类敏感性和高分辨率的谱系或进化枝分配,但现有的工具通常只能解决其中一项任务。我们开发了 Afanc 作为一个可扩展框架,连接数据库构建、分类反卷积和病原体测序数据的概率谱系分配。Afanc 源代码可在 https://github.com/ArthurVM/Afanc 获取。结果:Afanc 集成了自动参考数据库构建、参考质量和代表性过滤、Kraken 2 报告反卷积、竞争性映射、变异调用和基于贝叶斯 SNP 的分类。我们评估了分枝杆菌科物种和亚种分配、混合分枝杆菌科数据集、结核分枝杆菌谱系分类、恶性疟原虫地理谱系预测和 猴痘 谱系分类的 Afanc。Afanc 解析了所有模拟覆盖范围 队列研究(包括 1% 基因组 宽度)的分枝杆菌科分类群,并从混合数据集中恢复了所有预期分类群。结核病,Afanc 以统一的分辨率分配了 955/958 个样本,没有错误的分支调用。在恶性疟原虫中,Afanc 将 18,830/20,712 个样本分配给区域谱系,比 Malaria-Profiler 好 15.8 个百分点。对于 猴痘,Afanc 没有产生错误的分支分配,并实现了 99.4% 的元数据分辨率协调分类。结论:Afanc 提供了一个可重用的框架,用于从嘈杂的广泛分类学证据转向跨不同病原体系统的可解释的谱系水平推断。通过链接数据库管理、分类反卷积和基于概率标记的分类,Afanc 支持病原体 基因组监测 工作流程,其中必须在下游流行病学或进化解释之前明确处理混合分类信号、不完整证据和谱系模糊性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
可靠的病原体 基因组监测 需要广泛的分类敏感性和高分辨率的谱系或进化枝分配,但现有工具通常只能解决其中一项任务。该研究旨在开发一个框架,整合这些功能以改进病原体 监测。
设计与对象
该研究评估了分枝杆菌科物种和亚种分配、混合分枝杆菌科数据集、结核分枝杆菌谱系分类、恶性疟原虫地理谱系预测和 猴痘 谱系分类的 Afanc。
核心方法
Afanc 集成了自动参考数据库构建、参考质量和代表性过滤、Kraken 2 报告反卷积、竞争性映射、变异调用和基于贝叶斯 SNP 的分类。
主要结果
Afanc 解析了所有模拟覆盖范围 队列研究(包括 1% 基因组 宽度)的分枝杆菌科分类群,并从混合数据集中恢复了所有预期分类群。对于 猴痘,Afanc 没有产生错误的分支分配,并实现了 99.4% 的元数据分辨率协调分类。
解释与创新
Afanc 提供了一个可重用的框架,用于从嘈杂的广泛分类学证据转向跨不同病原体系统的可解释的谱系水平推断。
科研与公卫意义
Afanc 支持病原体 基因组监测 工作流程,其中混合分类信号、不完整证据和谱系模糊性必须在下游流行病学或进化解释之前明确处理。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告局限性或证据强度。

Afanc: taxonomic deconvolution and probabilistic lineage assignment for pathogen genomic surveillance

Authors: Arthur V. Morris, Anna Price, Thomas R. Connor
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background: Reliable pathogen genomic surveillance requires both broad tax- onomic sensitivity and high-resolution lineage or clade assignment, yet existing tools usually address only one of these tasks. We developed Afanc as an extensible framework that connects database construction, taxonomic deconvolution and probabilistic lineage assignment from pathogen sequencing data. Afanc source code is available at https://github.com/ArthurVM/Afanc. Results: Afanc integrates automated reference database construction, reference- quality and representativeness filtering, Kraken 2 report deconvolution, com- petitive mapping, variant calling and Bayesian SNP-based classification. We evaluated Afanc across Mycobacteriaceae species and subspecies assignment, mixed Mycobacteriaceae datasets, Mycobacterium tuberculosis lineage classifi- cation, Plasmodium falciparum geolineage prediction and Mpox lineage clas- sification. Afanc resolved Mycobacteriaceae taxa across all simulated coverage cohorts, including 1% genome breadth, and recovered all expected taxa from mixed datasets. tuberculosis, Afanc assigned 955/958 samples at har- monised resolution with no wrong-branch calls. falciparum, Afanc assigned 18,830/20,712 samples to regional lineages, outperforming Malaria-Profiler by 15.8 percentage points. For Mpox, Afanc produced no wrong-branch assignments and achieved 99.4% metadata-resolution-harmonised classification. Conclusions: Afanc provides a reusable framework for moving from noisy broad taxonomic evidence to interpretable lineage-level inference across diverse pathogen systems. By linking database curation, taxonomic deconvolution and probabilistic marker-based classification, Afanc supports pathogen genomic surveillance workflows in which mixed taxonomic signal, incomplete evidence and lineage ambiguity must be handled explicitly before downstream epidemiological or evolutionary interpretation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Reliable pathogen genomic surveillance requires both broad taxonomic sensitivity and high-resolution lineage or clade assignment, yet existing tools usually address only one of these tasks. The study aimed to develop a framework that integrates these capabilities for improved pathogen surveillance.
Design and population
The study evaluated Afanc across Mycobacteriaceae species and subspecies assignment, mixed Mycobacteriaceae datasets, Mycobacterium tuberculosis lineage classification, Plasmodium falciparum geolineage prediction and Mpox lineage classification.
Core methods
Afanc integrates automated reference database construction, reference-quality and representativeness filtering, Kraken 2 report deconvolution, competitive mapping, variant calling and Bayesian SNP-based classification.
Main results
Afanc resolved Mycobacteriaceae taxa across all simulated coverage cohorts, including 1% genome breadth, and recovered all expected taxa from mixed datasets. For Mpox, Afanc produced no wrong-branch assignments and achieved 99.4% metadata-resolution-harmonised classification.
Interpretation and novelty
Afanc provides a reusable framework for moving from noisy broad taxonomic evidence to interpretable lineage-level inference across diverse pathogen systems.
Scientific and public-health significance
Afanc supports pathogen genomic surveillance workflows in which mixed taxonomic signal, incomplete evidence and lineage ambiguity must be handled explicitly before downstream epidemiological or evolutionary interpretation.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations or evidence strength.
