具有确定性热空间的空间编码光子微球透镜阵列使光流控表面增强拉曼散射芯片能够动态监测多个生物标记物。

Spatially Encoded Photonic Microsphere Lens Arrays with Deterministic Hotspaces Enabling Optofluidic Surface-Enhanced Raman Scattering Chip for Dynamic Monitoring of Multiple Biomarkers.
作者： Zhenyong Dong, Xiaoxian Liu, Tao Wu, Feixiang Zheng, Yukai Liu, Xiao Ren, Feng Xu, Lunbiao Cui, Yan-Qing Lu, Rui Xiao
摘要中文翻译临床迫切需要对多种生物标志物进行实时、定量动态监测;然而,传统技术受到繁琐的工作流程和耗时的操作程序的限制。表面增强拉曼散射 (SERS) 具有高灵敏度和分子指纹识别能力,但其在动态监测中的应用受到信号不稳定的限制,信号不稳定是由于随机热点分析物空间不匹配引起的信号不可再现性,以及对端点测量的完全依赖,从而妨碍了动力学信息提取。因此,我们提出了一种光流控 SERS 芯片,将空间编码光子微球透镜阵列 (SE-PMLA) 与确定性热空间集成,以克服这些限制。通过将金纳米粒子精确集成到微球透镜表面,每个功能单元实现了光子纳米喷射、回音壁模式和局域表面等离子体共振之间的级联光子等离子体耦合,并通过近场反馈增强将随机热点转变为工程化的均匀热空间,提供2.8×1010的平均增强因子和相对标准偏差低于5%的信号均匀性。利用空间编码传感,我们展示了对甲型/乙型流感病毒、腺病毒和猴痘病毒的单样本同步高通量检测,检测限分别达到 0.53 pg/mL、128.85 拷贝/mL、199.56 拷贝/mL 和 2.7 pg/mL。至关重要的是,该芯片的超稳定信号输出支持基于初始结合率的动力学定量模型,揭示了分析物浓度与其初始结合率(即斜率)之间的线性相关性。该方法实现了60至120秒内的快速、高精度动态监测,同时将线性检测范围扩大了两个数量级。最终,该芯片为实时病原体筛查和危重疾病诊断提供了强大的分钟级护理解决方案。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
临床迫切需要对多种生物标志物进行实时、定量动态监测;然而,传统技术受到繁琐的工作流程和耗时的操作程序的限制。
设计与对象
该研究提出了一种光流控 SERS 芯片,将空间编码光子微球透镜阵列 (SE-PMLA) 与确定性热空间集成,以克服病原体动态监测的局限性。
核心方法
该芯片将金纳米粒子集成到微球透镜表面,以实现光子纳米喷射、回音壁模式和局部表面等离子体共振之间的级联光子等离子体耦合,并通过近场反馈增强将随机热点转变为工程化的均匀热空间。
主要结果
该芯片利用空间编码传感技术,对甲型/乙型流感病毒、腺病毒和猴痘病毒进行单样本同步高通量检测,检测限分别达到 0.53 pg/mL、128.85 拷贝/mL、199.56 拷贝/mL 和 2.7 pg/mL。
解释与创新
至关重要的是,该芯片的超稳定信号输出支持基于初始结合率的动力学定量模型,揭示了分析物浓度与其初始结合率(即斜率)之间的线性相关性。
科研与公卫意义
该方法实现了60至120秒内的快速、高精度动态监测,同时将线性检测范围扩大了两个数量级。
局限与证据强度
提供的证据并未报告研究的局限性或证据强度。

Spatially Encoded Photonic Microsphere Lens Arrays with Deterministic Hotspaces Enabling Optofluidic Surface-Enhanced Raman Scattering Chip for Dynamic Monitoring of Multiple Biomarkers.

Authors: Zhenyong Dong, Xiaoxian Liu, Tao Wu, Feixiang Zheng, Yukai Liu, Xiao Ren, Feng Xu, Lunbiao Cui, Yan-Qing Lu, Rui Xiao
Original abstract (English)
Real-time, quantitative dynamic monitoring of multiple biomarkers is urgently needed in clinical practice; however, conventional technologies are limited by cumbersome workflows and time-consuming operational procedures. Surface-enhanced Raman scattering (SERS) offers high sensitivity and molecular fingerprinting capability, but its application in dynamic monitoring is restricted by signal instability caused by signal irreproducibility arising from stochastic hotspot analyte spatial mismatches, and an exclusive reliance on endpoint measurements that precludes kinetic information extraction. Hence, we present an optofluidic SERS chip integrating spatially encoded photonic microsphere lens arrays (SE-PMLAs) with deterministic hotspaces to overcome these limitations. Through precise integration of gold nanoparticles onto microsphere lens surfaces, each functional unit achieves cascaded photonic-plasmonic coupling among photonic nanojets, whispering-gallery modes, and localized surface plasmon resonance, with near-field feedback reinforcement that transforms random hotspots into engineered, uniform hotspaces, delivering an average enhancement factor of 2.8 × 1010 and signal uniformity with a relative standard deviation below 5%. Using spatially encoded sensing, we demonstrate single-sample, synchronous high-throughput detection of influenza A/B viruses, adenovirus, and monkeypox virus, achieving limits of detection of 0.53 pg/mL, 128.85 copies/mL, 199.56 copies/mL, and 2.7 pg/mL, respectively. Crucially, the chip's ultrastable signal output enables a kinetic quantification model based on the initial binding rate, revealed a linear correlation between analyte concentration and its initial binding rate (i.e., slope). This approach achieves rapid, high-precision dynamic monitoring within 60 to 120s while expanding the linear detection range by two orders of magnitude. Ultimately, this chip delivers a robust, minute-level point-of-care solution for real-time pathogen screening and critical disease diagnostics.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Real-time, quantitative dynamic monitoring of multiple biomarkers is urgently needed in clinical practice; however, conventional technologies are limited by cumbersome workflows and time-consuming operational procedures.
Design and population
The study presents an optofluidic SERS chip integrating spatially encoded photonic microsphere lens arrays (SE-PMLAs) with deterministic hotspaces to overcome limitations in dynamic monitoring of pathogens.
Core methods
The chip integrates gold nanoparticles onto microsphere lens surfaces to achieve cascaded photonic-plasmonic coupling among photonic nanojets, whispering-gallery modes, and localized surface plasmon resonance, with near-field feedback reinforcement that transforms random hotspots into engineered, uniform hotspaces.
Main results
Using spatially encoded sensing, the chip demonstrated single-sample, synchronous high-throughput detection of influenza A/B viruses, adenovirus, and monkeypox virus, achieving limits of detection of 0.53 pg/mL, 128.85 copies/mL, 199.56 copies/mL, and 2.7 pg/mL, respectively.
Interpretation and novelty
Crucially, the chip's ultrastable signal output enables a kinetic quantification model based on the initial binding rate, revealed a linear correlation between analyte concentration and its initial binding rate (i.e., slope).
Scientific and public-health significance
This approach achieves rapid, high-precision dynamic monitoring within 60 to 120s while expanding the linear detection range by two orders of magnitude.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations or evidence strength for the study.

สรปการตรวจสอบขาวการระบาด ของโรค/ภยสขภาพในรอบสปดาหท 40 小时。2569

สรปการตรวจสอบขาวการระบาดของโรค/ภยสขภาพในรอบสปดาหท 40 ป พ.ศ. 2569
作者： ทีมเฝ้าระวังเหตุการณ์ผิดปกติ
摘要中文翻译กรมควบคุมโรคได้รับรายงานเหตุการณ์โรค และภัยสุขภาพที่สําคัญ ผ่านโปรแกรมการตรวจสอบข่าวการระบาด กรมควบคุมโรค จากเครือข่ายงานสาธารณสุขทั่วประเทศดังนี้ 1. 1. 1. 1. 1. 1. อาการผิดปกติเป็นกลุ่มก้อนภาย หลังบริโภคผลิตภัณฑ์อาหารเสริม จํานวน 104 ราย อําเภอนาน้อย จังหวัดน่าน 2. โรคฝีดาษวานร (猴痘) เป็นกลุ่มก้อน จํานวน 3 ราย เสียชีวิต 1 ราย 3. การประเมินความเสี่ยงโรคฝีดาษวานร (猴痘 Clade Ib) ในจังหวัดอุดรธานีสถานการณ์ต่างประเทศ กัมพูชายืนยันผู้ป่วยติดเชื้อไข้หวัดนก H5N1 2569 年 6 月
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
提供的证据并未报告具体的研究问题或知识差距;它提供了泰国疾病控制部门每周例行流行病学 监测 总结。
设计与对象
这是泰国疾病控制部通过国家公共卫生网络汇编全国报告的疾病和健康事件的每周流行病学 监测 报告(佛历 2569 年第 40 周)。
核心方法
该报告利用疾病控制部门的流行病监测程序从全国公共卫生网络收集和核实重要疾病和健康事件的报告;提到了乌隆他尼 猴痘 Clade Ib 的风险评估,但没有详细的方法论。
主要结果
报告了四个关键事件:(1)难府Na Noi地区出现104例食用补充剂后出现异常症状的聚集性病例;(2) 乌隆府发生 猴痘(猴痘)聚集性病例,共有 3 例病例,其中 1 例死亡;(3) 对乌隆他尼的 猴痘 Clade Ib 进行风险评估;(4)柬埔寨确认2026年第6例人类H5N1禽流感病例。
解释与创新
所提供的证据并未报告作者的解释或陈述超出原始 监测 计数和提及的 Clade Ib 风险评估的新颖性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果提供了关于在乌隆他尼发生致命结果的 猴痘 分支 Ib 集群、南府一个大型食品补充剂相关疾病集群以及区域 H5N1 活动的可操作 监测 数据,为立即的公共卫生应对和风险评估优先事项提供信息。
局限与证据强度
证据仅限于抽象的 监测 总结,没有方法细节、分母数据、案例定义或统计分析;病例数少(3 例 猴痘 病例,1 例死亡)和缺乏实验室或流行病学关联数据限制了信心,需要进行低证据强度评估。

สรปการตรวจสอบขาวการระบาดของโรค/ภยสขภาพในรอบสปดาหท 40 ป พ.ศ. 2569

Authors: ทีมเฝ้าระวังเหตุการณ์ผิดปกติ
Original abstract (English)
กรมควบคุมโรค ได้รับรายงานเหตุการณ์ โรค และภัยสุขภาพที่สําคัญ ผ่านโปรแกรมการตรวจสอบข่าวการระบาด กรมควบคุมโรค จากเครือข่ายงานสาธารณสุขทั่วประเทศ ดังนี้ สถานการณ์ในประเทศ 1. อาการผิดปกติเป็นกลุ่มก้อนภายหลังบริโภคผลิตภัณฑ์อาหารเสริม จํานวน 104 ราย อําเภอนาน้อย จังหวัดน่าน 2. โรคฝีดาษวานร (Mpox) เป็นกลุ่มก้อน จํานวน 3 ราย เสียชีวิต 1 ราย จังหวัดอุดรธานี 3. การประเมินความเสี่ยงโรคฝีดาษวานร (Mpox Clade Ib) ในจังหวัดอุดรธานี สถานการณ์ต่างประเทศ กัมพูชายืนยันผู้ป่วยติดเชื้อไข้หวัดนก H5N1 รายที่ 6 ในปี 2569
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The supplied evidence does not report a specific research question or knowledge gap; it presents a routine weekly epidemiological surveillance summary from the Thai Department of Disease Control.
Design and population
This is a weekly epidemiological surveillance report (week 40, Buddhist year 2569) from the Department of Disease Control, Thailand, compiling nationally reported disease and health events through the national public health network.
Core methods
The report uses the Department of Disease Control's epidemic surveillance program to collect and verify reports of important diseases and health events from the nationwide public health network; a risk assessment for Mpox Clade Ib in Udon Thani is mentioned without methodological detail.
Main results
Four key events are reported: (1) a cluster of 104 cases with abnormal symptoms after consuming food supplements in Na Noi district, Nan province; (2) an Mpox (monkeypox) cluster of 3 cases with 1 death in Udon Thani province; (3) a risk assessment for Mpox Clade Ib in Udon Thani; and (4) Cambodia's confirmation of its 6th human H5N1 avian influenza case in 2026.
Interpretation and novelty
The supplied evidence does not report author interpretation or stated novelty beyond the raw surveillance counts and the mention of a Clade Ib risk assessment.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide actionable surveillance data on an Mpox Clade Ib cluster with a fatal outcome in Udon Thani, a large food-supplement-associated illness cluster in Nan, and regional H5N1 activity, informing immediate public health response and risk assessment priorities.
Limitations and evidence strength
Evidence is limited to an abstract-only surveillance summary with no methodological detail, denominator data, case definitions, or statistical analysis; the small case numbers (3 Mpox cases, 1 death) and lack of laboratory or epidemiological linkage data constrain confidence, warranting a low evidence strength assessment.

特考韦瑞 对人类 猴痘病毒 感染的 治疗 的功效:随机对照试验的系统回顾和荟萃分析。

Efficacy of Tecovirimat for the Treatment of Mpox Virus Infection in Humans: Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis of Randomized Controlled Trials.
作者： Davi M Grecco, Luciano Araldi, Giordano C Soldera, João A B Zimmermann, João V Brisolara, Giovanna A Artero, Luiza F Streher, Maria E F Turatti, Harapan Harapan
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述评估了 特考韦瑞 对人类 猴痘病毒 感染的 治疗 的有效性和安全性,重点关注临床结果,例如皮肤病变临床消退的时间、第 28-29 天的病变消退以及 28 天全因 死亡率。
证据基础与方法
对随机对照试验 (RCT) 进行了系统回顾和荟萃分析,检索了从开始到 2026 年 6 月 5 日的 PubMed、Embase 和 Cochrane 对照试验中央注册库。使用 RoB 2 评估偏倚风险,并使用 GRADE 评估证据的确定性。使用具有限制最大似然估计和 Hartung-Knapp-Sidik-Jonkman 调整的随机效应模型汇总危险比 (HR) 和风险比 (RR)。
共识与结论
Tecovirimat did not significantly shorten the time to clinical resolution of skin lesions compared with placebo (HR 1.10, 95% CI 0.52-2.31, GRADE: moderate) or increase lesion resolution by day 28-29 (RR 1.00, 95% CI 0.92-1.08, GRADE: high). Virological clearance, pain-related outcomes, and serious adverse events were also comparable between treatment groups.
争议与缺口
未观察到统计异质性(两种结果的 I 2 = 0%)。然而,第 28 天的 死亡率 在这两项试验中都很罕见,排除了荟萃分析。该综述强调需要进一步的证据来确定选定的高危人群是否可以从 抗病毒药物 治疗中受益。
科研与实践启示
目前的随机证据不支持常规使用 特考韦瑞 来加速 猴痘 患者的临床恢复或提高 猴痘 率。需要进一步研究来探索其对特定高危人群的潜在益处。

Efficacy of Tecovirimat for the Treatment of Mpox Virus Infection in Humans: Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis of Randomized Controlled Trials.

Authors: Davi M Grecco, Luciano Araldi, Giordano C Soldera, João A B Zimmermann, João V Brisolara, Giovanna A Artero, Luiza F Streher, Maria E F Turatti, Harapan Harapan
Original abstract (English)
Tecovirimat has been widely used for the treatment of mpox despite limited evidence supporting its efficacy. We conducted a systematic review and meta-analysis of randomized controlled trials (RCTs) to evaluate the efficacy and safety of tecovirimat for this purpose. PubMed, Embase, and the Cochrane Central Register of Controlled Trials were searched from inception to June 5, 2026 for RCTs comparing tecovirimat with placebo or standard care in patients with laboratory-confirmed mpox. Primary outcomes were time to clinical resolution of skin lesions, clinical resolution by day 28-29, and 28-day all-cause mortality. Hazard ratios (HRs) and risk ratios (RRs) were pooled using random-effects models with restricted maximum likelihood estimation and Hartung-Knapp-Sidik-Jonkman adjustment. Risk of bias was assessed using RoB 2 and certainty of evidence using GRADE. Two low-risk-of-bias RCTs involving 941 participants were included. Tecovirimat did not shorten the time to clinical resolution of skin lesions compared with placebo (HR 1.10, 95% CI 0.52-2.31, GRADE: moderate) or increase the lesion resolution by day 28-29 (RR 1.00, 95% CI 0.92-1.08, GRADE: high). No statistical heterogeneity was observed (I 2 = 0% for both outcomes). Mortality at day 28 was rare in both trials, precluding meta-analysis. Virological clearance, pain-related outcomes, and serious adverse events were also comparable between treatment groups. In conclusion, current randomized evidence does not support routine tecovirimat use to accelerate clinical recovery or improve mortality rates in patients with mpox, while further evidence is needed to determine whether selected high-risk populations may benefit from antiviral therapy.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review evaluates the efficacy and safety of tecovirimat for the treatment of mpox virus infection in humans, focusing on clinical outcomes such as time to clinical resolution of skin lesions, lesion resolution by day 28-29, and 28-day all-cause mortality.
Evidence base and method
A systematic review and meta-analysis of randomized controlled trials (RCTs) were conducted, searching PubMed, Embase, and the Cochrane Central Register of Controlled Trials from inception to June 5, 2026. Risk of bias was assessed using RoB 2, and certainty of evidence was evaluated using GRADE. Hazard ratios (HRs) and risk ratios (RRs) were pooled using random-effects models with restricted maximum likelihood estimation and Hartung-Knapp-Sidik-Jonkman adjustment.
Consensus and conclusions
Tecovirimat did not significantly shorten the time to clinical resolution of skin lesions compared with placebo (HR 1.10, 95% CI 0.52-2.31, GRADE: moderate) or increase lesion resolution by day 28-29 (RR 1.00, 95% CI 0.92-1.08, GRADE: high). Virological clearance, pain-related outcomes, and serious adverse events were also comparable between treatment groups.
Controversies and gaps
No statistical heterogeneity was observed (I 2 = 0% for both outcomes). However, mortality at day 28 was rare in both trials, precluding meta-analysis. The review highlights the need for further evidence to determine whether selected high-risk populations may benefit from antiviral therapy.
Research and practice implications
Current randomized evidence does not support routine tecovirimat use to accelerate clinical recovery or improve mortality rates in patients with mpox. Further research is needed to explore its potential benefits in specific high-risk populations.

布隆迪 猴痘 的社区知识和对社会、生态和行为风险因素的看法:全国横断面调查

Community knowledge and perceptions of social, ecological, and behavioural risk factors for mpox in Burundi: A nationwide cross-sectional survey
作者： Néhémie Nzoyikorera, Emmanuelline Mukama, Jonas Ngendakumana, Ferdinand Nsengimana, Roger Ciza, Marie Noelle Uwineza, Alain Tamba Yombouno, Duncan Kabiito Matovu, Rodgers Rodriguez Ayebare, Cathy Asiimwe Tabaro
摘要中文翻译猴痘 仍然是非洲的一个重要公共卫生问题,特别是在 Ib 分支在几个国家传播之后。在布隆迪,关于人口知识、风险认知、预防行为和接受 猴痘 疫苗接种 的意愿的信息有限。本研究评估了普通人群中与 猴痘 准备情况相关的社会和行为因素。我们于 2025 年 3 月至 12 月期间在布隆迪的五个省份进行了全国横断面调查。使用改编自非洲疾病预防控制中心 猴痘 行为和社会科学框架的结构化调查问卷,对总共 5,177 名 15 岁及以上的参与者进行了采访。未应用抽样权重;因此,调查结果应解释为受调查参与者的估计,而不是全国加权的人口估计。进行单变量分析来描述关键变量,同时使用带有广义估计方程的修正泊松回归模型进行双变量和多变量分析,以确定与猴痘知识和预防和控制实践相关的因素。总体而言,大多数参与者听说过 猴痘(94.7%)。许多人正确地将皮疹(94.4%)和发烧(84.3%)视为常见症状,并且大多数人认为直接接触是 传播 的一种模式(86.6%)。然而,21.4% 的参与者错误地将细菌识别为病原体,24.5% 的参与者不知道病原体。广播是主要的信息来源(82.0%),也是最值得信赖的来源(87.0%)。更高的收入与更好的 猴痘 知识相关。预防和控制做法因知识水平、收入、婚姻状况和艾滋病毒状况而异。尽管大多数参与者 (86.1%) 不知道 疫苗 的可用性,但 76.5% 的参与者表示愿意接受 疫苗接种(如果有)。尽管接受 疫苗接种 的意识和意愿很高,但 猴痘 知识仍然存在重大差距,特别是在低收入群体中。加强可及且有针对性的风险沟通策略对于改善布隆迪的 猴痘 准备工作至关重要。

使用改编自非洲疾病预防控制中心 猴痘 行为和社会科学框架的结构化调查问卷,对总共 5,177 名 15 岁及以上的参与者进行了采访。
共识与结论
更高的收入与更好的 猴痘 知识相关。
争议与缺口
在布隆迪,关于人口知识、风险认知、预防行为和接受 猴痘 疫苗接种 的意愿的信息有限。
科研与实践启示
尽管接受 疫苗接种 的意识和意愿很高,但 猴痘 知识仍然存在重大差距,特别是在低收入群体中。

Community knowledge and perceptions of social, ecological, and behavioural risk factors for mpox in Burundi: A nationwide cross-sectional survey

Authors: Néhémie Nzoyikorera, Emmanuelline Mukama, Jonas Ngendakumana, Ferdinand Nsengimana, Roger Ciza, Marie Noelle Uwineza, Alain Tamba Yombouno, Duncan Kabiito Matovu, Rodgers Rodriguez Ayebare, Cathy Asiimwe Tabaro
Original abstract (English)
Mpox remains an important public health concern in Africa, particularly following the spread of clade Ib in several countries. In Burundi, limited information is available on population knowledge, risk perception, preventive behaviours, and willingness to accept mpox vaccination. This study assessed social and behavioural factors associated with mpox preparedness among the general population. We conducted a nationwide cross-sectional survey between March and December 2025 across five provinces of Burundi. A total of 5,177 participants aged 15 years and older were interviewed using a structured questionnaire adapted from the Africa CDC behavioural and social sciences framework for mpox. Sampling weights were not applied; therefore, findings should be interpreted as estimates among surveyed participants rather than nationally weighted population estimates. Univariate analyses were conducted to describe key variables, while bivariate and multivariable analyses using modified Poisson regression models with generalized estimating equations were performed to identify factors associated with mpox knowledge and prevention and control practices. Overall, most participants had heard about mpox (94.7%). Many correctly identified skin rash (94.4%) and fever (84.3%) as common symptoms, and most recognized direct contact as a mode of transmission (86.6%). However, 21.4% of participants incorrectly identified bacteria as the causative agent and 24.5% did not know the causative agent. Radio was the main source of information (82.0%) and the most trusted source (87.0%). Higher income was associated with better mpox knowledge. Prevention and control practices varied by knowledge level, income, marital status, and HIV status. Although most participants (86.1%) were unaware of the availability of a vaccine, 76.5% reported willingness to accept vaccination if available. Despite high awareness and willingness to accept vaccination, important gaps in mpox knowledge remain, particularly among lower-income groups. Strengthening accessible and targeted risk communication strategies will be essential to improve mpox preparedness in Burundi.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Sampling weights were not applied; therefore, findings should be interpreted as estimates among surveyed participants rather than nationally weighted population estimates.
Evidence base and method
A total of 5,177 participants aged 15 years and older were interviewed using a structured questionnaire adapted from the Africa CDC behavioural and social sciences framework for mpox.
Consensus and conclusions
Higher income was associated with better mpox knowledge.
Controversies and gaps
In Burundi, limited information is available on population knowledge, risk perception, preventive behaviours, and willingness to accept mpox vaccination.
Research and practice implications
Despite high awareness and willingness to accept vaccination, important gaps in mpox knowledge remain, particularly among lower-income groups.

同行评审报告:病例报告:在 暴发 期间进行临床试验:猴痘 病例报告 [版本 2;同行评审:1 项批准,1 项保留批准]

Peer Review Report For: Case Report: Conducting Clinical Trials during an Outbreak: An Mpox case report [version 2; peer review: 1 approved, 1 approved with reservations]
作者： Isabella Kyohairwe, Mackline Hope, Herman Mwanja, Diana Namuddu, Irene Mbabazi, Ruth Nabisere Mirembe, Allan Kengo, Reuben Kiggundu, Christine Sekaggya-Wiltshire, Hope Mackline
摘要中文翻译背景:在传染病 暴发 期间进行临床试验提出了操作、伦理和方法学方面的挑战。2024-2025 年乌干达 猴痘 暴发 期间,在一项以 HIV 为重点的临床试验中发生了一起 猴痘 病例,需要立即采取操作和伦理反应。该试验旨在评估治疗药物监测的可行性并优化艾滋病毒感染者的抗菌治疗。病例介绍 我们报告了一名成年男性艾滋病毒感染者的病例,该男子参加了乌干达一家三级艾滋病毒诊所的艾滋病毒感染者抗菌药物治疗药物监测 (TAP) 试验。在开始抗菌治疗和研究入组后,在预定的研究随访中,他出现了涉及面部和上肢的水疱脓疱性皮疹。研究人员迅速启动感染防控措施,暂停研究程序,并通知国家监测团队。通过实时聚合酶链反应检测证实了猴痘感染。参与者被转移到指定的隔离设施进行临床管理并退出试验。通过远程(电话)依从性咨询和抗逆转录病毒药物补充协调来维持艾滋病毒护理的连续性。经过 21 天的自我监测后,研究参与者或人员中没有发现继发病例。结论 本报告强调了 暴发 准备、适应性风险管理以及与公共卫生团队快速协调的重要性,以保护参与者和人员、保护研究完整性并确保流行病期间基本临床护理的连续性。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
在传染病 暴发 期间进行临床试验提出了操作、伦理和方法学方面的挑战。病例报告 重点关注乌干达一项针对 HIV 的临床试验中的 猴痘 病例。
证据基础与方法
病例报告 描述了 2024-2025 年 猴痘 暴发 在乌干达进行的一项以 HIV 为重点的临床试验中对 猴痘 病例的操作和伦理反应。
共识与结论
该试验旨在评估治疗药物监测的可行性并优化艾滋病毒感染者的抗菌治疗。经过 21 天的自我监测后,研究参与者或人员中没有发现继发病例。
争议与缺口
病例报告 强调了 暴发 准备、适应性风险管理以及与公共卫生团队快速协调的重要性,以保护参与者和人员、保护研究完整性并确保流行病期间基本临床护理的连续性。该报告没有提及任何具体争议或证据差距。
科研与实践启示
本报告强调需要强有力的 暴发 准备、适应性风险管理以及与公共卫生团队的快速协调,以确保临床试验期间参与者和人员的安全。它还强调了在流行病期间保持基本临床护理连续性的重要性。

Peer Review Report For: Case Report: Conducting Clinical Trials during an Outbreak: An Mpox case report [version 2; peer review: 1 approved, 1 approved with reservations]

Authors: Isabella Kyohairwe, Mackline Hope, Herman Mwanja, Diana Namuddu, Irene Mbabazi, Ruth Nabisere Mirembe, Allan Kengo, Reuben Kiggundu, Christine Sekaggya-Wiltshire, Hope Mackline
Original abstract (English)
Background: Conducting clinical trials during infectious disease outbreaks presents operational, ethical, and methodological challenges. During the 2024–2025 Mpox outbreak in Uganda, an Mpox case occurred within an HIV-focused clinical trial requiring immediate operational and ethical response. The trial aimed to assess the feasibility of therapeutic drug monitoring and optimize antimicrobial therapy in people living with HIV. Case presentation We report the case of an adult man living with HIV enrolled in the Therapeutic Drug Monitoring for Antimicrobial Agents for People Living with HIV (TAP) trial at a tertiary HIV clinic in Uganda. Following initiation of antimicrobial therapy and study enrolment, at a scheduled study follow-up visit, he developed a vesiculopustular rash involving the face and upper limbs. Research staff promptly activated infection prevention and control measures, halted study procedures, and notified the national surveillance team. Mpox infection was confirmed by real-time polymerase chain reaction testing. The participant was transferred to a designated isolation facility for clinical management and withdrawn from the trial. Continuity of HIV care was maintained through remote (phone) adherence counselling and coordination of antiretroviral refills. No secondary cases were identified among study participants or personnel after 21 days of self-monitoring. Conclusions This report highlights the importance of outbreak preparedness, adaptive risk management, and rapid coordination with public health teams to safeguard participants and personnel, protect research integrity, and ensure continuity of essential clinical care during epidemics.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Conducting clinical trials during infectious disease outbreaks presents operational, ethical, and methodological challenges. The case report focuses on an Mpox case within an HIV-focused clinical trial in Uganda.
Evidence base and method
The case report describes the operational and ethical response to an Mpox case within an HIV-focused clinical trial during the 2024–2025 Mpox outbreak in Uganda.
Consensus and conclusions
The trial aimed to assess the feasibility of therapeutic drug monitoring and optimize antimicrobial therapy in people living with HIV. No secondary cases were identified among study participants or personnel after 21 days of self-monitoring.
Controversies and gaps
The case report highlights the importance of outbreak preparedness, adaptive risk management, and rapid coordination with public health teams to safeguard participants and personnel, protect research integrity, and ensure continuity of essential clinical care during epidemics. The report does not mention any specific controversies or evidence gaps.
Research and practice implications
This report emphasizes the need for robust outbreak preparedness, adaptive risk management, and rapid coordination with public health teams to ensure the safety of participants and personnel during clinical trials. It also underscores the importance of maintaining continuity of essential clinical care during epidemics.

КОНТРОЛЬ КАЧЕСТВА И СТАБИЛЬНОСТЬ ВАКЦИН ПРОТИВ 猴痘: СОВРЕМЕННЫЕ КИТЕРИИ И 猴痘 疫苗 的质量控制和稳定性:现代标准和方法

Academic paper (English original; verified English title unavailable)
作者： E. Шаяхметов, А. Жақыпбек, K. Жугунисов, К. Джекебеков, Г. Шыныбекова, Л. Кутумбетов, Б. Мырзахметова, К.К. Джекебеков, К. Жугунисов, Е. А. Шаяхметов
摘要中文翻译本文根据世界卫生组织(WHO)的建议、欧亚经济联盟(EAEU)、国际协调委员会(ICH)和哈萨克斯坦共和国的监管框架,对目前猴痘 疫苗的稳定性评估和质量控制要求进行了比较分析。讨论了免疫生物学产品稳定性测试的主要方法,包括长期和加速研究、其目标、测试条件及其在建立 疫苗 保质期中的作用。该审查系统化了疫苗稳定性评估中使用的主要质量属性,包括理化参数(外观、pH和均质性)、生物学特性(感染滴度、抗原含量、免疫原性和遗传稳定性)、安全指标(毒性、热原性和回复毒力)和微生物纯度。结果表明,偏离建议的储存温度、暴露于光和湿气以及冷链中断可能会导致抗原成分降解和 疫苗 免疫原性下降。另外还关注当前可用的 猴痘 疫苗 的特性,包括 ACAM2000、JYNNEOS、LC16m8 和 OrthopoxVac,重点是 疫苗 平台、复制属性和存储条件。分析表明,国际和区域监管要求总体上是一致的;然而,稳定性研究方法和质量控制程序仍然存在某些差异。研究结果强调需要进一步完善国家监管框架和实施评估 疫苗 生物活性的先进方法。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
此次审查的重点是猴痘 疫苗的质量控制和稳定性,审查了世界卫生组织(WHO)、欧亚经济联盟(EAEU)、国际协调理事会(ICH)和哈萨克斯坦共和国等国际和区域监管机构推荐的标准和方法。核心问题是这些要求如何影响 疫苗 的稳定性和质量保证。
证据基础与方法
该审查是对 疫苗 稳定性测试和质量控制的现行标准和实践的系统分析。它讨论了稳定性测试的主要方法,包括长期和加速研究、其目标、测试条件及其在确定 疫苗 保质期中的作用。该证据基于监管机构的建议和现有 猴痘 疫苗 的特征。
共识与结论
尽管稳定性研究方法和程序存在差异,但国际和地区对 猴痘 疫苗 稳定性和质量控制的监管要求总体上是一致的。它还强调了理化、生物、安全和微生物参数在疫苗稳定性评估中的重要性。
争议与缺口
该评论指出,偏离推荐的储存条件,例如暴露于光线、潮湿或温度波动,可能会导致抗原降解和免疫原性降低。然而,它没有提供对这些影响的具体机制或程度的详细分析。此外,审查强调需要进一步改进国家监管框架和实施评估 疫苗 生物活性的先进方法。
科研与实践启示
研究结果表明需要加强国家监管框架并采用先进方法来评估 疫苗 生物活性。这包括改善储存条件并监测疫苗在各种环境因素下的稳定性,以确保其功效和安全性。

Academic paper (English original; verified English title unavailable)

Original title (English)
КОНТРОЛЬ КАЧЕСТВА И СТАБИЛЬНОСТЬ ВАКЦИН ПРОТИВ MPOX: СОВРЕМЕННЫЕ КРИТЕРИИ И ПОДХОДЫ QUALITY CONTROL AND STABILITY OF MPOX VACCINES: MODERN CRITERIA AND APPROACHES
Authors: E. Шаяхметов, А. Жақыпбек, K. Жугунисов, К. Джекебеков, Г. Шыныбекова, Л. Кутумбетов, Б. Мырзахметова, К.К. Джекебеков, К. Жугунисов, Е. А. Шаяхметов
Original abstract (English)
This article presents a comparative analysis of current requirements for stability assessment and quality control of mpox vaccines in accordance with the recommendations of the World Health Organization (WHO), the regulatory framework of the Eurasian Economic Union (EAEU), the International Council for Harmonisation (ICH), and the Republic of Kazakhstan. The main approaches to stability testing of immunobiological products are discussed, including long-term and accelerated studies, their objectives, testing conditions, and their role in establishing vaccine shelf life. The review systematizes the principal quality attributes used in vaccine stability assessment, including physicochemical parameters (appearance, pH, and homogeneity), biological characteristics (infectious titer, antigen content, immunogenicity, and genetic stability), safety indicators (toxicity, pyrogenicity, and reversion to virulence), and microbiological purity. It is shown that deviations from recommended storage temperatures, as well as exposure to light and moisture and disruptions in the cold chain, may lead to degradation of antigenic components and a decline in vaccine immunogenicity. Additional attention is given to the characteristics of currently available mpox vaccines, including ACAM2000, JYNNEOS, LC16m8, and OrthopoxVac, with emphasis on vaccine platform, replicative properties, and storage conditions. The analysis demonstrated that international and regional regulatory requirements are generally harmonized; however, certain differences remain in approaches to stability studies and quality control procedures. The findings highlight the need for further improvement of the national regulatory framework and the implementation of advanced methods for assessing vaccine biological activity.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on the quality control and stability of mpox vaccines, examining the criteria and approaches recommended by international and regional regulatory bodies such as the World Health Organization (WHO), the Eurasian Economic Union (EAEU), the International Council for Harmonisation (ICH), and the Republic of Kazakhstan. The central question is how these requirements influence vaccine stability and quality assurance.
Evidence base and method
The review is a systematic analysis of current standards and practices for vaccine stability testing and quality control. It discusses the main approaches to stability testing, including long-term and accelerated studies, their objectives, testing conditions, and their role in determining vaccine shelf life. The evidence is based on the recommendations of regulatory bodies and the characteristics of available mpox vaccines.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that international and regional regulatory requirements for mpox vaccine stability and quality control are generally harmonized, although differences exist in approaches to stability studies and procedures. It also highlights the importance of physicochemical, biological, safety, and microbiological parameters in vaccine stability assessment.
Controversies and gaps
The review notes that deviations from recommended storage conditions, such as exposure to light, moisture, or temperature fluctuations, may lead to antigen degradation and reduced immunogenicity. However, it does not provide a detailed analysis of the specific mechanisms or the extent of these effects. Additionally, the review emphasizes the need for further improvements in national regulatory frameworks and the implementation of advanced methods for assessing vaccine biological activity.
Research and practice implications
The findings suggest the need for enhanced national regulatory frameworks and the adoption of advanced methods for evaluating vaccine biological activity. This includes improving storage conditions and monitoring the stability of vaccines under various environmental factors to ensure their efficacy and safety.

撒哈拉以南非洲地区医护人员应对猴痘大流行的准备情况:快速回顾调查知识差距

Healthcare Workers’ Preparedness for Monkeypox Pandemic Response in SubSaharan Africa: A Rapid Review Investigating the Knowledge Gap
作者： Arole Darwin Touko, Ruth Tabi Amin, Gordon Takop Nchanji
摘要中文翻译背景:猴痘 是一种人畜共患病毒性疾病,由撒哈拉以南非洲 (SSA) 流行的 正痘病毒 引起,该病的死灰复燃凸显了改善卫生保健工作者 (HCW) 准备情况的迫切需要。尽管“同一个健康”方法具有潜力,但目前的实施仍然分散。本快速审查综合了 HCW 的知识、态度、信息来源以及 SSA 中 猴痘 准备障碍的证据,以确定培训需求和政策影响。方法:按照系统评价和荟萃分析的首选报告项目 (PRISMA) 指南,使用 MeSH 术语和布尔运算符对 2000 年至 2024 年间发表的研究进行 PubMed 和 ScienceDirect 搜索。纳入了评估 HCW 知识、态度或 SSA 中 猴痘 准备情况的研究,并提供英文或法文全文;那些涉及非医护人员人群、在 SSA 之外进行或缺乏主要数据的研究被排除在外。两名评审员使用标准化模板独立筛选和提取数据;使用 JBI 分析横断面研究批判性评估清单评估质量。结果:纳入了来自 388 份记录的五项研究(喀麦隆、埃塞俄比亚、尼日利亚、刚果民主共和国;n = 1,672 名医护人员)。只有 46.8% 的医护人员表现出足够的 猴痘 知识。障碍包括对社交媒体的依赖和获得正规培训的机会有限。对预防的积极态度与较高的知识水平相关。结论:尽管该地区的病死率接近全球平均水平的十倍,但只有不到一半的 SSA 医护人员表现出足够的 猴痘 知识;积极态度并未转化为临床准备,反映了严重的意向与行动差距;社交媒体作为信息来源占据主导地位,强调了在同一个健康培训框架内需要经过同行评审的替代方案。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
猴痘 是一种人畜共患病毒性疾病,由撒哈拉以南非洲 (SSA) 流行的 正痘病毒 引起,该病的死灰复燃凸显了卫生保健工作者 (HCW) 迫切需要加强做好准备。
证据基础与方法
遵循系统评价和荟萃分析的首选报告项目 (PRISMA) 指南,使用 MeSH 术语和布尔运算符对 2000 年至 2024 年间发表的研究进行 PubMed 和 ScienceDirect 搜索。纳入了评估 HCW 知识、态度或 SSA 中 猴痘 准备情况的研究,并提供英文或法文全文;那些涉及非医护人员人群、在 SSA 之外进行或缺乏主要数据的研究被排除在外。两名评审员使用标准化模板独立筛选和提取数据;使用 JBI 分析横断面研究批判性评估清单评估质量。
共识与结论
纳入了来自 388 份记录的五项研究(喀麦隆、埃塞俄比亚、尼日利亚、刚果民主共和国;n = 1,672 名医护人员)。只有 46.8% 的医护人员表现出足够的 猴痘 知识。障碍包括对社交媒体的依赖和获得正规培训的机会有限。对预防的积极态度与较高的知识水平相关。

尽管该地区的病死率接近全球平均水平的十倍,但只有不到一半的 SSA 医护人员表现出足够的 猴痘 知识;积极态度并未转化为临床准备,反映了严重的意向与行动差距;社交媒体作为信息来源占据主导地位,强调了在同一个健康培训框架内需要经过同行评审的替代方案。
科研与实践启示
本快速审查综合了 HCW 的知识、态度、信息来源以及 SSA 中 猴痘 准备障碍的证据,以确定培训需求和政策影响。

Healthcare Workers’ Preparedness for Monkeypox Pandemic Response in SubSaharan Africa: A Rapid Review Investigating the Knowledge Gap

Authors: Arole Darwin Touko, Ruth Tabi Amin, Gordon Takop Nchanji
Original abstract (English)
Background: Mpox is a zoonotic viral disease caused by an Orthopoxvirus endemic to sub-Saharan Africa (SSA), where its resurgence underscores the urgent need for improved preparedness among health care workers (HCWs). Despite the potential of a One Health approach, current implementations remain fragmented. This rapid review synthesises evidence on HCWs' knowledge, attitudes, information sources, and barriers to preparedness regarding Mpox in SSA to identify training needs and policy implications. Methods: Following Preferred Reporting Items for Systematic Reviews and Meta-Analyses (PRISMA) guidelines, PubMed and ScienceDirect were searched using MeSH terms and Boolean operators for studies published between 2000 and 2024. Studies assessing HCW knowledge, attitudes, or preparedness for Mpox in SSA with full-text availability in English or French were included; those involving non-HCW populations, conducted outside SSA, or lacking primary data were excluded. Two reviewers independently screened and extracted data using a standardised template; quality was assessed using the JBI Critical Appraisal Checklist for Analytical CrossSectional Studies. Results: Five studies from 388 records were included (Cameroon, Ethiopia, Nigeria, DRC; n = 1,672 HCWs). Only 46.8% of HCWs demonstrated adequate Mpox knowledge. Barriers included reliance on social media and limited access to formal training. Positive attitudes towards prevention correlated with higher knowledge levels. Conclusion: Fewer than half of SSA HCWs demonstrate adequate Mpox knowledge despite a regional case fatality rate nearly ten times the global average; positive attitudes are not translating into clinical preparedness, reflecting a critical intention-action gap; and social media dominates as an information source, underscoring the need for peer-reviewed alternatives within a One Health training framework.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Mpox is a zoonotic viral disease caused by an Orthopoxvirus endemic to sub-Saharan Africa (SSA), where its resurgence underscores the urgent need for improved preparedness among health care workers (HCWs).
Evidence base and method
Following Preferred Reporting Items for Systematic Reviews and Meta-Analyses (PRISMA) guidelines, PubMed and ScienceDirect were searched using MeSH terms and Boolean operators for studies published between 2000 and 2024. Studies assessing HCW knowledge, attitudes, or preparedness for Mpox in SSA with full-text availability in English or French were included; those involving non-HCW populations, conducted outside SSA, or lacking primary data were excluded. Two reviewers independently screened and extracted data using a standardised template; quality was assessed using the JBI Critical Appraisal Checklist for Analytical Cross-Sectional Studies.
Consensus and conclusions
Five studies from 388 records were included (Cameroon, Ethiopia, Nigeria, DRC; n = 1,672 HCWs). Only 46.8% of HCWs demonstrated adequate Mpox knowledge. Barriers included reliance on social media and limited access to formal training. Positive attitudes towards prevention correlated with higher knowledge levels.
Controversies and gaps
Fewer than half of SSA HCWs demonstrate adequate Mpox knowledge despite a regional case fatality rate nearly ten times the global average; positive attitudes are not translating into clinical preparedness, reflecting a critical intention-action gap; and social media dominates as an information source, underscoring the need for peer-reviewed alternatives within a One Health training framework.
Research and practice implications
This rapid review synthesises evidence on HCWs' knowledge, attitudes, information sources, and barriers to preparedness regarding Mpox in SSA to identify training needs and policy implications.

C 补充信息学术文献 · 综述C supplementaryAcademic literature · Review

生物信息学驱动的 基因组监测 和新兴病毒病原体的进化追踪

Bioinformatics-driven genomic surveillance and evolutionary tracking of emerging viral pathogens
作者： Taru Singh, Gorangi Mudgal, Chandra Shekhar
摘要中文翻译新出现的病毒病原体对全球健康构成持续威胁,这使得 基因组监测 和进化追踪成为重要的公共卫生工具。本综述评估了 基因组监测、病原体追踪和实时进化生物学的生物信息学工具、管道和分析方法,评估了每种工具的优势、局限性和病原体特异性适用性。使用跨病原体比较框架,我们研究了五个案例研究:SARS-CoV-2、埃博拉病毒、猴痘病毒(MPXV)、禽流感病毒和蜜蜂病毒,每个案例都需要全 基因组 测序、多序列比对、系统发育树构建和分子钟重建的定制组合,以重建 传播 链和进化动力学。讨论的主要工具包括 Nextstrain、GISAID、BEAST、IQ-TREE、MEGA 和 Pangolin。这些方法推动了切实的公共卫生成果,从针对 SARS-CoV-2 的 mRNA 疫苗 开发到针对禽流感的通用 疫苗 设计工作,但其影响仍然受到测序基础设施、专业知识和计算资源获取不均匀的限制。在所有五个案例研究中,没有一个管道能够普遍表现最佳:工具和平台的选择必须与每种病原体的生物学和给定环境中可用的 监测 基础设施相匹配。在全球范围内,特别是在资源贫乏地区加强这种能力,对于确保及时发现和描述新出现的病原体以采取有效的公共卫生应对措施至关重要。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述重点关注基因组监测和新兴病毒病原体的进化追踪,特别是猴痘病毒(正痘病毒猴痘)、其进化枝(进化枝I 猴痘病毒、进化枝II 猴痘病毒、进化枝Ib)和疾病猴痘。核心问题是如何应用生物信息学工具和方法来监测和了解这些病毒的基因 进化,从而为公共卫生应对措施提供信息。
证据基础与方法
该综述采用跨病原体比较框架来评估 基因组监测、病原体追踪和实时进化生物学的生物信息学工具、管道和分析方法。它包括五个案例研究:SARS-CoV-2、埃博拉病毒、猴痘病毒(MPXV)、禽流感病毒和蜜蜂病毒。每个案例研究都需要全 基因组 测序、多序列比对、系统发育树构建和分子钟重建的定制组合。
共识与结论
基因组监测 和 猴痘病毒 及其分支的进化跟踪有助于理解 传播 链和进化动力学。这些方法为切实的公共卫生成果做出了贡献,例如针对 SARS-CoV-2 的 mRNA 疫苗 开发和针对禽流感的通用 疫苗 设计工作,尽管它们在 猴痘 上的应用是针对病原体的。
争议与缺口
该审查强调,没有单一的生物信息学管道在普遍情况下表现最佳,工具和平台的选择必须根据 猴痘病毒 和 监测 可用基础设施的生物学特性进行定制。此外,这些方法对 猴痘 的影响受到测序基础设施、专业知识和计算资源访问不均匀的限制。
科研与实践启示
加强 基因组监测 的 基因组监测 能力,特别是在资源贫乏地区,对于确保及时检测和表征新出现的病原体至关重要。这包括改善测序基础设施、专业知识和计算资源的获取,以支持有效的公共卫生应对措施。

Bioinformatics-driven genomic surveillance and evolutionary tracking of emerging viral pathogens

Authors: Taru Singh, Gorangi Mudgal, Chandra Shekhar
Original abstract (English)
Emerging viral pathogens pose a persistent threat to global health, making genomic surveillance and evolutionary tracking essential public health tools. This review evaluates bioinformatics tools, pipelines, and analytical approaches for genomic surveillance, pathogen tracking, and real-time evolutionary biology, assessing the strengths, limitations, and pathogen-specific applicability of each. Using a cross-pathogen, comparative framework, we examine five case studies: SARS-CoV-2, Ebola virus, monkeypox virus (MPXV), avian influenza virus, and honeybee viruses, each requiring a tailored combination of whole-genome sequencing, multiple-sequence alignment, phylogenetic tree building, and molecular clock reconstruction to reconstruct transmission chains and evolutionary dynamics. Key tools discussed include Nextstrain, GISAID, BEAST, IQ-TREE, MEGA, and Pangolin. These approaches have driven tangible public health outcomes, from mRNA vaccine development for SARS-CoV-2 to universal vaccine design efforts for avian influenza, but their impact remains constrained by uneven access to sequencing infrastructure, expertise, and computational resources. Across all five case studies, no single pipeline performs best universally: tool and platform choice must instead be matched to each pathogen’s biology and the surveillance infrastructure available in a given setting. Strengthening this capacity globally, particularly in under-resourced regions, is critical to ensuring emerging pathogens are detected and characterized in time for an effective public health response.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on the genomic surveillance and evolutionary tracking of emerging viral pathogens, specifically the mpox virus (Orthopoxvirus monkeypox), its clades (clade I mpox virus, clade II mpox virus, clade Ib), and the disease mpox. The central question is how bioinformatics tools and methods can be applied to monitor and understand the genetic evolution of these viruses to inform public health responses.
Evidence base and method
The review employs a cross-pathogen, comparative framework to evaluate bioinformatics tools, pipelines, and analytical approaches for genomic surveillance, pathogen tracking, and real-time evolutionary biology. It includes five case studies: SARS-CoV-2, Ebola virus, monkeypox virus (MPXV), avian influenza virus, and honeybee viruses. Each case study requires a tailored combination of whole-genome sequencing, multiple-sequence alignment, phylogenetic tree building, and molecular clock reconstruction.
Consensus and conclusions
Genomic surveillance and evolutionary tracking of the mpox virus and its clades have been instrumental in understanding transmission chains and evolutionary dynamics. These methods have contributed to tangible public health outcomes, such as mRNA vaccine development for SARS-CoV-2 and universal vaccine design efforts for avian influenza, though their application to mpox is pathogen-specific.
Controversies and gaps
The review highlights that no single bioinformatics pipeline performs best universally, and tool and platform choice must be tailored to the biology of the mpox virus and the surveillance infrastructure available. Additionally, the impact of these methods on mpox is constrained by uneven access to sequencing infrastructure, expertise, and computational resources.
Research and practice implications
Strengthening genomic surveillance capacity for the mpox virus, particularly in under-resourced regions, is critical to ensuring timely detection and characterization of emerging pathogens. This includes improving access to sequencing infrastructure, expertise, and computational resources to support effective public health responses.

B routine priorityPublic-health news

Mpox outbreak hits California county as health officials probe US spread - New York Post

News Brief
A recent mpox outbreak has been confirmed in Stanislaus County, California, with four cases of Clade I mpox reported. Three of the infected individuals had no recent international travel, prompting concerns about potential community transmission within the US. The California Department of Public Health has confirmed two infections as Clade I, while further testing is ongoing for the remaining two cases. Health officials emphasize that the risk to the general public remains low, though they are actively investigating how the virus may be spreading locally. The outbreak highlights a shift in mpox transmission patterns, as cases with no international travel history are increasing. Officials are urging at-risk individuals to get vaccinated with JYNNEOS and to seek medical care if symptoms arise. The exact transmission route for the cases in Stanislaus County is still under investigation, and the broader implications of this trend are being closely monitored.

俄罗斯猴痘分支保密阻碍全球风险模型,第三例莫斯科病例出现于多莫杰多沃 - 技术时报

Russia's Mpox Clade Secrecy Blocks Global Risk Models as Third Moscow Case Hits Domodedovo - Tech Times
新闻简报一名男子被确认感染猴痘病毒,并被送往莫斯科州多莫杰多沃医院住院治疗,标志着俄罗斯本月第三例确诊病例,以及自2022年暴发疫情以来的第八例确诊病例。这一病例凸显了俄罗斯疾病报告中的关键漏洞:所有八例确诊病例均未提供分支数据,这使得全球卫生机构无法判断该病毒株是否属于更严重的Ib分支,该分支已在23个欧盟和欧洲经济区国家传播,或是较轻的IIb分支,该分支与2022年的全球暴发疫情相关。多莫杰多沃医院服务于来自中亚、东南亚和非洲的国际航线,2026年已处理多起猴痘病例,包括2月份的首例确诊病例和4月初至少一例。缺乏分支数据意味着这些病例对全球健康构成的风险无法准确评估,而俄罗斯联邦消费者权益保护和人道主义事务局(Rospotrebnadzor)并未提供相关信息。世界卫生组织指出,俄罗斯的疾病报告实践仍然不透明,例如2026年10月对西伯利亚实验室工作人员疑似鼠疫死亡的延迟披露。这种保密模式并非首次出现,历史上曾有1959年天花暴发疫情被压制,以及1979年斯维尔德洛夫斯克炭疽事件的延迟承认。目前该情况仍在调查中,俄罗斯确认的病例中尚未报告死亡。
查看新闻五要素
时间
第三例莫斯科地区猴痘病例于2026年11月1日被确认。俄罗斯2026年的首例确诊病例于2026年2月2日报告,且在2026年4月初至少报告了一例额外病例。2022年的暴发疫情被作为历史背景提及,世界卫生组织关于Ib分支的公共卫生紧急事件(PHEIC)已于2025年9月5日结束。
地点与对象
该病例发生在莫斯科州的多莫杰多沃医院。该医院位于多莫杰多沃国际机场附近,该机场提供来自中亚、东南亚和非洲的国际航线。莫斯科州和圣彼得堡已被确认为出现猴痘病例的地区,且该医院在2026年已处理多起病例。
事件
一名男子被确认感染猴痘病毒,并被送往莫斯科州多莫杰多沃医院住院治疗,标志着俄罗斯在该月内第三例莫斯科地区确诊病例,以及自2022年暴发疫情以来的第八例确诊病例。该事件由多莫杰多沃医院的官方通讯渠道报告。
规模、影响与风险
截至2026年10月,23个欧盟和欧洲经济区国家共记录了1,939例Ib分支病例,其中仅2026年9月就出现了342例。Ib分支被认为比IIb分支更为严重,其记录的病例死亡率低于1%。俄罗斯未披露分支数据,阻碍了对其病例对全球健康风险的准确评估。
应对、状态与不确定性
Rospotrebnadzor表示该情况处于持续控制之下,但这一保证伴随着延迟或最小化疾病披露的既定模式。缺乏分支数据意味着该机构无法确认病毒株的传播动态,世界卫生组织也指出俄罗斯的疾病报告实践仍然不透明。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Russia's Mpox Clade Secrecy Blocks Global Risk Models as Third Moscow Case Hits Domodedovo - Tech Times

News Brief
A man has been confirmed positive for mpox and hospitalized at Domodedovo Hospital in Moscow Oblast, marking Russia's third confirmed case in a single month and its eighth since the 2022 outbreak. The case highlights a critical gap in Russia's disease reporting: the lack of clade data for all eight confirmed cases prevents global health agencies from assessing whether the strain is clade Ib, which is more severe and has spread across 23 EU and EEA countries, or clade IIb, which is less severe and associated with the 2022 global outbreak. Domodedovo Hospital, which serves international routes from Central Asia, Southeast Asia, and Africa, has handled multiple mpox cases in 2026, including the first confirmed case in February and at least one in early April. The absence of clade data means that the risk posed by these cases cannot be accurately evaluated, and Russia's Federal Service for Consumer Rights Protection and Human Wellbeing (Rospotrebnadzor) has not provided this information. The WHO has noted that Russia's disease reporting practices remain opaque, as seen in the delayed disclosure of a Siberian lab worker's suspected plague death in October 2026. This pattern of secrecy is not new, with historical examples such as the suppression of a 1959 smallpox outbreak and the delayed acknowledgment of the 1979 Sverdlovsk anthrax incident. The situation remains under investigation, with no deaths reported among Russia's confirmed cases.
Original source text (English)
Medical workers wearing protective equipment wait in front of the gateway to enter the red zone to treat coronavirus patients at the Spasokukotsky clinical hospital in Moscow on April 22, 2020. (Photo by Yuri KADOBNOV / AFP) YURI KADOBNOV/AFP via Getty Images A man has been confirmed positive for mpox and hospitalized at Domodedovo Hospital in Moscow Oblast — Russia's third confirmed Moscow-region case in a single month and its eighth since the 2022 outbreak . That count, however, tells only part of the story that international epidemiologists need. For two years and across all eight confirmed cases, Russia has never disclosed which mpox clade its patients carry — a gap that prevents Europe's disease surveillance architecture from assessing whether Moscow is seeding the continent with the milder strain responsible for the 2022 global outbreak or the more dangerous clade Ib that has since spread across 23 EU and EEA countries . The case was confirmed by Domodedovo Hospital's official communications channel. All patients at the facility connected to Russia's 2026 mpox cluster have been reported in satisfactory condition and receiving routine medical care. No deaths have been reported among Russia's confirmed cases. But the absence of clade data means none of those reassuring data points can be fitted into the broader picture that Europe's public health agencies are trying to build. Domodedovo Is Russia's Recurring Mpox Flashpoint The November 1, 2026 hospitalization is not an isolated event at Domodedovo. The same hospital handled Russia's first confirmed 2026 mpox case, reported on February 2, 2026, and at least one additional case in early April 2026. In October 2026, the Moscow Oblast saw five hospitalized after suspected exposure , triggering an emergency epidemiological investigation. That same month, five cases were confirmed in St. Petersburg at Botkin Hospital. Domodedovo International Airport — one of Moscow's three major commercial airports — processed 13.9 million passengers in 2025, according to airport CEO Andrei Ivanov. The airport serves direct routes from Central Asia, Southeast Asia, and Africa — the regions with the highest ongoing rates of mpox transmission. The recurrence of cases processed through the same hospital, adjacent to one of those routes' primary arrival points, is a pattern epidemiologists outside Russia cannot yet formally characterize, because Russia has not provided the case-level data that would allow them to do so. What Russia Has Never Said About Its Mpox Strain Here is the specific gap at the center of this story: mpox virus (MPXV) circulates in two major clades, each subdivided. Clade IIb drove the 2022 global outbreak, primarily through sexual contact among men who have sex with men; it is the better-understood strain, generally considered less severe. Clade Ib emerged as the dominant threat in 2024, originating in Democratic Republic Congo . The World Health Organization declared a Public Health Emergency of International Concern for clade Ib on August 14, 2024 . That PHEIC ended September 5, 2025 — but clade Ib has not ended. As of October 2026, 23 EU and EEA countries have recorded clade I cases in the past 12 months, reaching 1,939 total clade I cases , with 342 cases in September 2026 alone. In hospitalized patients, clade Ib's case fatality rate below one percent is nonetheless documented as more severe than clade IIb, and its transmissibility profile outside endemic African countries is, in the words of Canada's Public Health Agency, "not yet fully understood." That phrase represents the scientific community's honest assessment of an ongoing epidemiological question. Russia's non-disclosure of clade data across all eight confirmed cases extends that uncertainty to its own territory and, by extension, to every country with a Domodedovo flight path. The European Centre for Disease Prevention and Control's surveillance system covers 23 EU and EEA member states. Russia is neither an EU member nor an EEA participant, so its cases are structurally excluded from ECDC reporting . ECDC cannot fill in Russia's clade blank — that can only come from Rospotrebnadzor, Russia's Federal Service for Consumer Rights Protection and Human Wellbeing, which has not provided it. "Traditional Values" as Epidemiological Strategy: What the Cases Say Back On August 19, 2024 — five days after the WHO declared its second mpox PHEIC — Rospotrebnadzor chief Anna Popova offered Russia's official posture on the threat. "In Russia with its traditional values this disease, which is an epidemic disease, is not something we need to be afraid of," Popova told Russian media . At the time, Rospotrebnadzor had confirmed three total mpox cases in Russia. The implication was clear: Russia's cultural conservatism and the predominance of heterosexual relationships would insulate the population from the outbreak driven by sexual transmission networks. Russia now has eight confirmed cases, three of them in a 30-day window in the Moscow region. None of this disproves Popova's broader point that Russia's epidemic profile differs from Western Europe's — but the cases at Domodedovo are not arising from domestic sexual transmission networks in the way the 2022 outbreak did in Western cities. They are appearing at or near an international airport serving routes from regions where both clade Ib and clade IIb are actively circulating. Without clade identification, Rospotrebnadzor cannot confirm what Popova's "traditional values" framing implicitly assumed: that the strain arriving at Domodedovo is the one that primarily spreads through sexual contact networks, rather than the one whose transmission dynamics outside Africa remain incompletely characterized. Rospotrebnadzor has described the situation as under constant control , a phrase repeated across multiple mpox communications. That assurance sits alongside the agency's documented pattern of delayed or minimized disease disclosures on other pathogens. In October 2026, WHO Director-General Tedros Adhanom Ghebreyesus stated "we don't yet have the full picture" of a Siberian lab worker's death from suspected plague — because Russia had not proactively notified the WHO, and WHO learned case through media . Russia's Opacity Follows a Soviet-Era Template The October 2026 plague episode is not an anomaly in Russia's disease disclosure history — it is a recent iteration of a pattern with deep roots. In 1959, a Moscow smallpox outbreak sickened 46 people and killed three before authorities acknowledged it; the incident was suppressed during the Soviet era. In 1979, an anthrax outbreak in Sverdlovsk killed at least 68 people; Soviet officials blamed contaminated meat for 13 years before the KGB origin was acknowledged after the Soviet Union's collapse. After the 1986 Chernobyl disaster, evacuation of Pripyat was delayed 36 hours as the scale of the event was withheld from the public. "The system can silence, but it's not competent enough to coordinate, so it produces contradiction," said Judy Twigg at Virginia Commonwealth University, a political science professor who has studied Russia's public health governance and the COVID-19 data integrity question. Russia's COVID-19 death reporting drew sustained scrutiny: while Russia's official pandemic death toll stood at 338,091 through late 2021, 681,100 COVID deaths were attributable to the virus over the same period in Rosstat data. The mpox clade gap is a subtler form of the same dynamic — not a contested count but a category of data that simply does not appear. Russia Has IHR Obligations — but the IHR Has No Enforcement Teeth Russia is a WHO member state and signatory to the International Health Regulations (IHR), the legally binding treaty that requires member states to notify WHO within 24 hours of events assessed under Annex 2 as potentially constituting a public health emergency of international concern. The treaty was revised after China's 2003 SARS cover-up to include verification procedures that allow WHO to seek information from member states even when they have not self-notified. Those procedures, however, come with a structural weakness that experts have described as the IHR's defining limitation. "There is no mechanism to force countries to comply or share information," according to Alexandra Phelan Johns Hopkins Bloomberg School of Public Health. Lawrence Gostin Georgetown University, director of the WHO Center on Global Health Law, described Russia's response to the October 2026 plague case as illustrating "the good, the bad and the ugly of the IHR," adding that countries disregarding their notification duties amounts to "a blaring siren about how unprepared we are." WHO's most aggressive lever — Article 10(4), which allows sharing information with other member states if a country refuses collaboration — is a weak instrument used by WHO sparingly . In the context of mpox clade disclosure, the absence of a clade designation does not obviously violate the letter of IHR requirements, which focus on event notification rather than granular virological characterization. But it defeats the purpose: the IHR exists to enable international risk assessment, and risk assessment for mpox in 2026 requires clade data. "Timely, transparent information sharing under the regulations is needed," Tedros said in October 2026 — a statement that applies with equal force to the question of what strain is circulating in Russia's capital region. What Europe Cannot Know Because Russia Won't Say The practical consequence of Russia's clade non-disclosure is not theoretical. Public health agencies in neighboring countries — Kazakhstan, Ukraine, Georgia, the Baltic states, and others with regular Domodedovo flight connections — cannot determine whether the cases being managed at Domodedovo Hospital represent imported clade IIb arrivals (the 2022 outbreak strain, which burned through and largely subsided in non-endemic settings) or clade Ib (the ongoing European outbreak strain, still actively circulating in 23 EU and EEA countries as of fall 2026). Those two scenarios call for different surveillance postures, different communication strategies, and different assessment of risk for travelers. The ECDC's clade I risk assessment for October 2026 rated the threat as moderate for gbMSM in EU and EEA countries, with ongoing community transmission. That assessment was built on data from the 23 countries inside ECDC's reporting architecture. Russia's cases did not contribute to it. When a traveler flies from Domodedovo to Warsaw, Vienna, or Helsinki, the risk model that public health authority is working from has a Russia-shaped hole in it. What Travelers Through Domodedovo Should Know Mpox transmits through direct contact with infectious skin lesions, bodily fluids, or respiratory particles, and through contact with contaminated personal items . It is not spread casually through brief airborne exposure in transit — an infected traveler sitting in an airport departure lounge does not pose the same risk as one with whom another person has close sustained contact. The practical risk to a transit passenger moving through Domodedovo is low. That said, travelers with upcoming Domodedovo connections, particularly those with immunocompromising conditions or those who are unvaccinated against smallpox and its relatives, should be aware of the signs of mpox infection: fever, muscle aches, and swollen lymph nodes followed by a distinctive rash that may appear on the face, hands, chest, or genitals within one to five days of symptom onset. Those who develop these symptoms within three weeks of travel through an affected region should contact a healthcare provider and disclose their travel history rather than presenting to an emergency room without warning. MVA-BN (marketed in the U.S. as Jynneos), the mpox vaccine, is authorized for pre-exposure prophylaxis in high-risk individuals. Travelers who believe they have a specific elevated risk exposure profile — including those who recently had skin-to-skin contact with someone who later tested positive — can discuss post-exposure vaccination with a healthcare provider. Russia Has Not Disclosed Clade. That Is the Story. Russia's mpox response has followed an internally consistent logic: confirm enough cases to demonstrate the surveillance system functions, keep case counts low enough to avoid triggering international scrutiny, invoke cultural framing to explain why the epidemiological threat is limited, and withhold the virological detail — clade — that would allow anyone outside Russia to independently assess whether that explanation is correct. Twigg's formulation about Russia's broader disease disclosure posture applies directly: the system silences the detail that would allow contradiction. Rospotrebnadzor knows what clade its patients carry — Vector Center, Russia's state virology institute with BSL-4 capacity, has the sequencing capability. The absence of clade disclosure from Russia's public communications is not a technical limitation. It is a decision. That decision leaves a gap in the European mpox surveillance picture that will persist until Russia changes course or the WHO finds a lever more effective than the one it currently holds. In the meantime, international epidemiologists are modeling clade Ib's spread across a continent whose eastern boundary, for these purposes, does not include Russia. Frequently Asked Questions What is the difference between mpox clade Ib and clade IIb, and why does it matter for Russia's cases? Mpox virus circulates in two major clades: Clade I (further divided into Ia and Ib) originated in Central Africa and is associated with greater severity; Clade II (including IIb) is associated with the 2022 global outbreak that primarily spread through sexual networks among men who have sex with men. Clade Ib became the WHO's second Public Health Emergency of International Concern in August 2024, and as of fall 2026 is active in 23 EU and EEA countries . Clade Ib is more severe than clade IIb, though the case fatality rate in hospitalized patients is below one percent for both. The critical issue with Russia's non-disclosure is that these two clades call for different public health responses: clade IIb is well-characterized, relatively well-contained, and primarily associated with specific sexual transmission networks; clade Ib's transmission dynamics outside endemic African countries remain, per Canada's Public Health Agency , "not yet fully understood." Without Russia disclosing which strain its patients carry, neighboring countries cannot calibrate their surveillance posture appropriately. How many mpox cases has Russia officially confirmed, and has clade ever been disclosed? Russia has officially confirmed eight mpox cases since the 2022 global outbreak, according to regional reporting and health agency data . Three of these cases were confirmed in October and November 2026, all in the Moscow region. No Russian health authority communication reviewed for this article has disclosed the mpox clade for any of the eight cases. The agency's most prominent public statement on the matter — Rospotrebnadzor chief Anna Popova's August 2024 invocation of Russia's "traditional values" as protection against the disease — addressed transmission risk framing rather than virological characterization, as documented in Anna Popova's August 2024 statement . Is Russia required to report mpox clade information to the WHO? Russia is a WHO member state and signatory to the International Health Regulations, which require notification of events that may constitute international public health emergencies. The IHR does not explicitly require granular virological characterization such as clade designation — but it exists to enable international risk assessment, and risk assessment for mpox in 2026 requires clade data to be meaningful. Experts including Georgetown University's Lawrence Gostin have described the IHR as lacking an enforcement mechanism to compel disclosure. WHO's most aggressive available response — sharing a country's non-disclosure with other member states — is considered a weak instrument used by WHO sparingly . WHO Director-General Tedros Adhanom Ghebreyesus called for timely IHR sharing in October 2026 in the context of Russia's plague case handling — a standard Russia has not met for mpox clade disclosure. What practical risk do Domodedovo Airport travelers face, and what should they watch for? The risk of contracting mpox during ordinary transit through Domodedovo is low. Mpox spreads through direct contact with infectious lesions, bodily fluids, or contaminated personal items — not through casual brief airborne exposure in transit spaces. Travelers should monitor themselves for symptoms for up to three weeks after travel: fever, muscle aches, and swollen lymph nodes that precede a distinctive skin rash appearing on the face, hands, chest, or genitals are the primary signs. Anyone developing these symptoms after travel should contact a healthcare provider — not an emergency room — disclosing their travel history. Those at elevated risk, including immunocompromised individuals and those who had close sustained skin contact with an infected person, should discuss post-exposure prophylaxis options with a healthcare provider promptly

The third Moscow-region mpox case was confirmed on November 1, 2026. Russia's first confirmed 2026 mpox case was reported on February 2, 2026, and at least one additional case was reported in early April 2026. The 2022 outbreak is referenced as a historical context, and the WHO PHEIC for clade Ib ended on September 5, 2025.
Location and population
The case occurred in Moscow Oblast, specifically at Domodedovo Hospital. The hospital is located near Domodedovo International Airport, which serves routes from Central Asia, Southeast Asia, and Africa. The Moscow Oblast and St. Petersburg have been identified as regions with mpox cases, and the hospital has handled multiple cases in 2026.
Event
A man was confirmed positive for mpox and hospitalized at Domodedovo Hospital in Moscow Oblast, marking Russia's third confirmed Moscow-region case in a single month and its eighth since the 2022 outbreak. The event was reported by Domodedovo Hospital's official communications channel.
Scale, impact and risk
As of October 2026, 23 EU and EEA countries have recorded 1,939 total clade I cases, with 342 cases in September 2026 alone. Clade Ib is considered more severe than clade IIb, with a documented case fatality rate below one percent. Russia's non-disclosure of clade data prevents accurate assessment of the risk posed by its cases to global health surveillance.
Response, status and uncertainty
Rospotrebnadzor has described the situation as under constant control, but this assurance is accompanied by a documented pattern of delayed or minimized disease disclosures. The absence of clade data means that the agency cannot confirm the strain's transmission dynamics, and the WHO has noted that Russia's disease reporting practices remain opaque.

Stanislaus County Reports Four Mpox Cases, Two Clade I - Hoodline
新闻简报截至2026年10月30日,加利福尼亚州斯坦尼斯拉乌斯县确认了四例猴痘病例,其中两例被鉴定为更为严重的I型克隆株。该县卫生官员正与加利福尼亚州公共卫生部合作,监测局势并确定剩余两例病例的分类。尽管I型克隆株的死亡率率超过10%,但对一般公众的风险仍然非常低。卫生官员建议提高对症状的意识,对高风险人群进行疫苗接种,并对疑似病例采取隔离措施以防止进一步传播。据《莫德斯托蜜蜂报》报道,州卫生官员确认了两例I型克隆株病例,且所有四例感染均为近期发生。美国疾病控制与预防中心(CDC)指出,截至目前为止,全美26个州和波多黎各已报告近130例I型克隆株病例,且本地传播自8月起已发生。导致2022年全球暴发的II型克隆株在全国范围内仍以低水平持续传播。公共卫生指导建议避免密切接触,不共用个人物品,并在出现不明原因皮疹或疮口时寻求医疗帮助。

B routine priorityPublic-health news

Stanislaus County Reports Four Mpox Cases, Two Clade I - Hoodline

News Brief
Stanislaus County, California, confirmed four mpox cases as of October 30, 2026, with two identified as the more severe clade I strain. The county health officials are collaborating with the California Department of Public Health to monitor the situation and determine the classification of the remaining two cases. While the mortality rate for clade I is upward of 10%, the risk for the general public remains very low. Health officials recommend awareness of symptoms, vaccination for high-risk individuals, and isolation for suspected cases to prevent further spread. The Modesto Bee reported that state health officials confirmed the two clade I cases, and all four infections are recent. The CDC notes that nearly 130 clade I cases have been reported across 26 states and Puerto Rico, with local transmission occurring since August. Clade II, which caused the 2022 global outbreak, continues to spread at low levels nationally. Public health guidance emphasizes avoiding close contact, refraining from sharing personal items, and seeking medical care for unexplained rashes or sores.

The classification of the remaining two cases has not yet been identified, leaving open questions about exactly what variety of the virus local residents are dealing with. The Modesto Bee reports that California state health officials confirmed the two clade I cases, while Stanislaus County health officials reported all four as recent infections. Mpox, formerly known as monkeypox, is a rare viral infection that causes a painful or itchy rash along with flu-like symptoms, according to the Stanislaus County Health Services Agency. Despite the new cases, the agency said the current risk of mpox for the general public remains very low. Two Strains, Two Very Different Risk Profiles Clade I mpox may be more severe than clade II, according to KPBS, and the distinction matters because of how differently the two strains have behaved. Clade I carries a mortality rate upward of 10%, attributed to Johns Hopkins School of Public Health, while clade II carries a mortality rate of 1% to 4% by the same attribution. Clade II is the strain that spurred the global mpox outbreak in 2022. Clade I mpox cases have often been linked to international travel, though infections among people without foreign travel in the past three months have been increasing, raising concern among U.S. communities about local spread. Clade I outbreaks have been centered in Central and Eastern Africa and Western Europe. The California Department of Public Health previously confirmed three unrelated clade I cases in Long Beach and Los Angeles County in 2025, and all three of those patients were hospitalized. County Says It's Tracking the Situation Closely Stanislaus County is working with the California Department of Public Health to monitor the mpox situation, the county health agency said, as officials try to pin down the classification of the two unidentified cases. Dr. Thea Papasozomenos said the public risk remains low despite the four county cases, and advised that awareness of symptoms, vaccination for those at higher risk, and seeking medical care when needed can help protect people and prevent further spread. Nationally, the picture has been shifting. The CDC reports that nearly 130 clade I mpox cases have been reported across at least 26 states plus Puerto Rico as of October, and that imported clade I cases began increasing in frequency starting in March, with local transmission occurring since August — a pattern the agency says points to a likely transition toward sustained clade Ib transmission in the United States. A separate CDC report notes that subclade IIb transmission persists at low levels nationally, while subclade Ib has spread across roughly 40 countries and includes travel-associated cases here. What Mpox Looks Like and How It Spreads The most common symptom of mpox is lesions, according to the county health agency, though some people develop only a few skin sores without any other symptoms. Other symptoms can include a rash or sores resembling pimples or blisters, along with fever, chills, swollen lymph nodes, headache, body aches, and fatigue. The virus spreads mostly through close contact, skin-to-skin contact, or sexual activity, and it can also spread through contaminated bedding, towels, or clothes — though casual interactions are not known to spread it easily. Anyone who develops an unexplained rash or sores is urged to contact a healthcare provider to ask about mpox testing, which involves swabbing the rash or sores. People who suspect they have mpox should avoid close physical contact with others, refrain from sharing personal items, and isolate until all sores have healed and new skin has formed. Vaccine Guidance for Those at Higher Risk JYNNEOS, the vaccine available for mpox, is recommended for people at higher risk of exposure and is given in two doses four weeks apart, according to FOX5 Vegas, and it is expected to protect against both clade I and clade II infection and serious illness. The California Department of Public Health recommends vaccination for gay or bisexual men and other men who have sex with men, transgender or gender-diverse individuals, people living with HIV or taking HIV PrEP or doxy PEP, anyone exposed to mpox in the previous two weeks, and travelers heading to a country with clade I spread who anticipate sexual or intimate contact. People who have already received one dose are encouraged to complete the series, and the overall vaccination goal is increasing coverage in high-risk groups given the current outbreaks. Anyone unsure of their own risk is encouraged to discuss it with a healthcare provider. How Stanislaus County Compares to Other Regions Stanislaus County is far from alone in confronting new mpox activity this year. San Diego County reported 27 clade II cases in 2026, mostly among people who were not fully vaccinated, all involving men between 17 and 53 years old, according to KPBS. The outlet also notes that most clade I cases outside Africa are associated with travel, though some areas have reported local spread. In Nevada, the Southern Nevada Health District recently announced its first confirmed clade I case in a Clark County resident with no reported travel exposure, on top of 11 confirmed clade II cases reported since August, per the station's report. That same district has logged 365 mpox cases total in Clark County since its first case was reported back in June 2022. Separately, NEJM Evidence has documented a clade Ib outbreak across Central and Eastern Africa driving multiple travel-associated infections, including three unvaccinated people confirmed infected in October 2025. 斯坦尼斯劳斯县报告四例猴痘病例;三名患者无国际旅行记录 - 医疗日报

Four mpox cases reported in Stanislaus County; three patients had no international travel - Medical Daily
新闻简报截至2026年10月31日,美国加利福尼亚州斯坦尼斯劳斯县报告了四例猴痘病例。其中两例被确认为I系,这是猴痘病毒的一个更为严重的变种。值得注意的是,四名患者中有三名没有国际旅行史,这引发了对潜在本地传播传播的担忧。一名患者住院治疗,但官员表示对一般公众的风险仍然较低。其余两例的检测仍在进行中,同时正在进行接触者追踪以确定感染源。这与全国范围内的趋势一致,因为美国疾病控制与预防中心(CDC)注意到美国境内I系病例数量正在增加,且这些病例与国际旅行无明显关联,表明病毒可能正在向本地传播转变。这一情况凸显了持续监测和公共卫生措施的必要性,以应对病毒潜在的本地传播传播。

B routine priorityPublic-health news

Four mpox cases reported in Stanislaus County; three patients had no international travel - Medical Daily

News Brief
In Stanislaus County, California, four mpox cases were reported as of October 31, 2026. Two of these cases were confirmed as clade I, a more severe variant of the mpox virus. Notably, three of the four patients had no international travel history, raising concerns about potential local transmission. One patient was hospitalized, but officials stated the risk to the general public remains low. Testing is still ongoing for the remaining two cases, and contact tracing is being conducted to determine the source of infection. This aligns with a broader national trend, as the CDC has noted a growing number of clade I cases in the U.S. without international travel links, suggesting a possible shift toward domestic spread. The situation highlights the need for continued monitoring and public health measures to address potential local transmission of the virus.

Conditions Stanislaus County Mpox: Two Clade I Cases Confirmed as Three of Four Patients Had No Travel Abroad Published Oct 31, 2026 8:40 AM EDT By Dorothy Brooks KENA BETANCUR | AFP via Getty Images Share on Twitter Share on Facebook Share on WhatsApp Stanislaus County health officials have identified four recent mpox cases, and California state health officials have confirmed that two of them are clade I, the type of mpox that has historically caused more severe illness. Three of the four people had not traveled outside the country, the Modesto Bee reported on Oct. 29. Testing has not yet classified the other two cases, and officials are still looking into the fourth person's travel history. One person was hospitalized. The lack of travel is the key detail for Modesto-area residents. Clade I cases in the U.S. were long tied to travel abroad, so infections in people who have not left the country raise concern that the virus is spreading locally. Still, county officials say the risk to the general public is low. A Local Signal of Wider Spread Dr. Thea Papasozomenos, Stanislaus County's public health officer, said the "risk to the general public remains low." The Stanislaus cases fit a national pattern. Since August, a growing share of U.S. clade I cases have occurred in people with no recent international travel and no known link to a traveler, according to the CDC's clade I mpox situation summary. The agency says presumed domestic spread has been reported in more than 10 jurisdictions, which it calls a sign of a "likely transition to sustained clade Ib transmission in the United States." As of Oct. 14, the CDC counted more than 90 clade I cases in at least 23 states and Puerto Rico. The Modesto Bee has since reported almost 130 cases in 26 states and Puerto Rico. California saw this pattern first. In October 2025, state officials confirmed three unrelated clade I cases in Southern California, in Long Beach and Los Angeles County, among people who had not traveled internationally or had contact with one another. They were the first U.S. clade I cases not linked to travel. All three patients were hospitalized and were recovering with standard medical care, according to a California Department of Public Health health advisory. MedicalDaily previously reported that California issued a June health alert on rising clade I introductions. At that time, all seven of the state's 2026 clade I cases were linked to travel. The Stanislaus cases suggest that has changed. Clade I Compared With the 2022 Strain The 2022 global outbreak was driven by clade II, which mostly caused milder illness and spread largely through sexual networks among men who have sex with men. Clade I has historically caused more severe disease and has also spread through household contact in parts of Africa, including among children. Severity estimates vary. The Modesto Bee, citing the Johns Hopkins Bloomberg School of Public Health, reported clade I death rates of more than 10%, compared with 1% to 4% for clade II. Recent cases outside Africa have been milder. The CDC says travel-associated clade I cases in the U.S. have been almost entirely clade Ib, with no deaths and relatively mild illness in the available data, and that more than 300 clade Ib cases reported in high-income countries have resulted in no deaths. State health officials have also said clade I has not been shown to spread more easily than clade II. People with weakened immune systems, including those with poorly controlled HIV, face a higher risk of severe disease. Public reports have not described the patients' ages, how they were infected, or whether the Stanislaus cases are connected to each other or to cases elsewhere in California. Vaccine Eligibility for Higher-Risk Residents The CDC rates the risk to the general U.S. population as low and the risk to men who have sex with men as low to moderate. For most Modesto-area households, that means no change in routine is needed. People in higher-risk groups, however, can protect themselves with vaccination. Mpox spreads mainly through close skin-to-skin contact, including sex, and can also spread through shared bedding, towels, or clothing. Casual contact is not known to spread it easily. The California Department of Public Health recommends the two-dose JYNNEOS vaccine for gay, bisexual, and other men who have sex with men; transgender, nonbinary, and gender-diverse people; people with HIV or those taking HIV PrEP or doxy-PEP; and anyone exposed to mpox in the past two weeks. It is also recommended for travelers to countries where clade I is spreading who expect sexual or intimate contact there. The doses are given four weeks apart, and people who received only one dose should complete the series. JYNNEOS is expected to protect against both clade I and clade II. When Los Angeles County confirmed its locally acquired clade I case last year, health officer Dr. Muntu Davis said, "The identification of cases of clade I mpox, which may cause more severe illness than the more common clade II is concerning," LAist reported. The county urged people at high risk to get the two-dose vaccine. Stanislaus County has dealt with mpox before. The county's case count climbed to 22 during the 2022 outbreak, and the Health Services Agency added vaccination clinics in response. Residents with questions about vaccine access can call the Stanislaus County Immunization Clinic at 209-558-7700 or ask their health care provider. Symptoms and When to Seek Care Symptoms include a painful or itchy rash or sores, fever, chills, swollen lymph nodes, headache, body aches, and fatigue. Some people develop only a few sores, which can be mistaken for other infections. Anyone with a new, unexplained rash or sores should contact a health care provider about testing, which involves swabbing the sores, and avoid close contact with others until evaluated. People diagnosed with mpox should follow medical guidance and stay isolated until all sores have healed and new skin has formed. Covering sores and not sharing bedding or towels can help protect household members. People with weakened immune systems should seek care promptly if symptoms appear. Stanislaus County is working with the state to classify the remaining cases, and MedicalDaily will report any additional confirmed infections. Four recent cases, with two confirmed as clade I and two still awaiting classification. Did the patients travel abroad? Three had not traveled outside the country. Officials are still checking the fourth person's travel history. Is the general public at risk? County officials say the risk to the general public remains low. How is clade I different from the 2022 outbreak strain? Clade I has historically caused more severe illness than clade II, which drove the 2022 outbreak, though recent clade Ib cases outside Africa have generally been milder. How does mpox spread? Mainly through close skin-to-skin contact, including sex, and through shared bedding, towels, or clothing. Who should get vaccinated? Where can Stanislaus residents get information about the vaccine? They can call the Stanislaus County Immunization Clinic at 209-558-7700 or ask their health care provider.
